# v2.1 Abilities Unit A7b — hardening / wiring / cleanup: recon packet

*Rulings in force: closure table §B6 (CL-89…CL-113) and the A7 handoff's A7b section. New decision rows: closure table **§B7 (CL-114…CL-127), PENDING**. Scope: the A7b handoff's Command Center field notes (Sep 28): recon only; four finding groups; bounded seams; STOP.*

**Status: ✅ RECON RULED (Brandon, Sep 28): closure table §B7 CL-114…CL-127, recorded verbatim. The build runs in the approved order S0a–S17 (no S11b).** The rulings differ from the recommendations in three places: **CL-117** (modified: the icicle crits, its shards do not, and the icicle advances Calculated Strike once), **CL-121** (C2: the Everglow pulse keeps excluding arena bosses in v2.1; truthful copy only if needed; boss pulse tuning banked) and **CL-122** (gauntlet eligibility banked for v2.2; no S11b). Grounder/Relay is fixed at the two sites only.
- *Recon state (for the record):* the recon itself changed only docs: this packet, closure table §B7, and the A7b handoff's status row.
- **Baseline:** HEAD `a869967`. It is docs-only over `db0406e`, so production is byte-identical to A7a's final freeze 14 (`1e595c711b0fd861`; 29 of 30 frozen files match, the 30th being the A7 handoff's refreshed status row). Remote is current.
- **Method:** six parallel read-only lanes:
  - A1: the direct-hit damage chains;
  - A2: status lifetimes and vulnerability;
  - B: Chill/Shock wiring;
  - C: Carrier geometry;
  - D: boss reach and Boss Mode;
  - E: Growth, dormant code, debug seams, test hardening and tooling.
- **Verification:** every fact below was re-checked against source by Claude. Line numbers are at `a869967`; the older docs' numbers have drifted, so match on strings.
- **Preserved:** every accepted A7a contract (the offer predicate, the enabler edges, the Codex tally, the canon machinery, CL-112's Atlas status, 80 = 79 draftable + Panda). None of the proposed seams touches them except through the canon's normal regenerate-and-`--check` path.
- **Out of scope** (Command Center): Neutral redesign, the JSON catalog migration (CL-113), Atlas hardening (CL-112), art/FX, the stat-pick rebalance, and unrelated refactors. AudioManager stays closeout-only.

Abbreviations: **GS** `Sparkforge/Scenes/GameScene.swift` · **PS** `Sparkforge/Systems/PlayerStats.swift` · **UM** `Sparkforge/Systems/UpgradeManager.swift` · **GC** `Sparkforge/Config/GameConfig.swift` · **EN** `Sparkforge/Nodes/EnemyNode.swift` · **AG** `Sparkforge/Config/ArenaGeometry.swift`.

## Group 1 — behaviour fixes already ruled

Each is either a ruled repair or a defect against an existing ruling. Where a sub-question remains, the row names its §B7 decision; the rest of the item is buildable as ruled.

**G1.1 The gun's boss path charges The Hunter.**
- **Today:** `handleProjectileHitBoss` calls only `erasureRegisterHit()` (GS:12004). The enemy path calls both meters (GS:11904, 11906), and Red Smile's boss contact charges both through `chargeRedSmileHitMeters(.boss)` (GS:12316; `RedSmileState.swift:142-150`).
- **Fix:** add `apexRegisterAttack()` just before GS:12004, one line. The approved A7a copy (the T5 face "hits charge an execute pounce", and the detail's "shots, bat bites and Red Smile sweeps charge…") becomes literally true, so there is **no copy change**.
- **Effect:** in boss fights the pounce gauge also fills from gun hits, with a 0.2s stack cooldown. A pounce still only executes a boss at ≤10% HP.
- **Coverage:** a scene-boundary order check (see G3.4); mutants for deleting the call and for mis-ordering it.

**G1.2 Grounder and Relay Imp are not boss-class.**
- **Today:** both call `applyBossHazardDamage` with its `fromBossClass: Bool = true` default (GS:10231, GS:10842). Every existing definition makes boss-class the arena boss or a mini-boss: the contact classifier at GS:11535-11548, the Giantkiller copy, and the Anvilborn precedent at GS:5016.
- **Fix:** pass `false` at both sites. Optionally, also drop the default so every caller has to choose. That makes the 8 other call sites (all genuinely boss-class) spell out `true`, so it's left to the Reviewer's taste.
- **Effect:** only Giantkiller's −10% damage reduction stops applying to these two Arena 3 enemies. The Boss Mode ATK dial never reaches them, because they spawn only from the Coilworks wave table.
- **Coverage:** exact-line checks plus mutants.

**G1.3 CL-98 Storm Engine = the normal count + 2.**
- **Today:** GS:5484 fires the fixed `spreadShotCount = 3` (PS:703; set at UM:1025). The normal count is `1 + extraProjectiles`, and Scatter is its only writer.
- **Fix:** a pure `PlayerStats.volleyPelletCount(isSpreadVolley:)` plus a `GameConfig.Shock` bonus of 2. The spread volley then fires 3 at base, 4 with Scatter T1, 5 with T2 and 6 with T3.
- **Copy:** the synergy line ("Every 3rd shot fires a chaining spread") states no number, so the copy is unchanged; only the sidecar is updated.
- **Interactions:**
  - Polar Vortex T4 condenses per pellet, and Scatter isn't CL-93-blocked, so the icicle rate rises (Scatter T3: 3.7 → 4.7 icicles per 3 volleys).
  - The Blackhole and Shadow Edge volley counter is unaffected.
  - Per-pellet riders (Fracture, echoes, Calculated Strike) scale with the count.
- **Coverage:** executed shock-harness checks (normal counts [1,2,3,4], spread counts [3,4,5,6]) plus 5 mutants.

**G1.4 CL-101 Erasure arena band.**
- **Today:** `eventHorizonScale(arena:)` bands at `...10 / 11...20 / 21...30` (GC:893-900), but the caller passes the 0-based `arenaConfig.id` (GS:7360). Every band is off by one. There's no effect today, since ids 0-5 all get 0.5.
- **Fix:** `switch arena + 1`, plus the doc comment.
- **Sweep:** no other arena-band or 0-/1-based confusion exists (unlock, defeat registry, TitleScene and BossRegistry all checked).
- **Prerequisite:** `GameConfig.Erasure` is hand-mirrored in `tools/signature-draw-harness/Stubs.swift:123-126`, so the real enum can't be extracted beside it. See G3.8.
- **Coverage:** EH1 bands at ids 0-9 / 10-19 / 20-29 / 30+; EH2 an exact call-site line; 3 mutants.

**G1.5 CL-96 Rootbound.**
- **Today:** UM:1074 writes `terraSlow += 0.15`, and nothing reads it (PS:751, reset at PS:1342). The only reader of `GameConfig.Growth.enemySlow` is GS:9062.
- **Fix:** GS:9062 reads `effectiveSlow(playerStats.terraSlow)`. PS's declaration and `reset()` use the constant.
- **Plumbing:** the 10 PS-compiling harnesses need `Growth` extracted, and the Stubs mirror (Stubs.swift:36-42) lacks `enemySlow` (G3.8).
- **Copy:** unchanged. "Cultivated ground grips harder — enemies on it are slower" is already true for the ruled behaviour (catalog CP4 pins it).
- **Effect:** the ground slow goes 30% → 45% at Growth ×3. **With Chill ×3 doubling slow potency, Growth ×3 + Chill ×3 now reaches the 80% cap** (60% today). Shatter eligibility is unchanged.
- **Coverage:** a `GW` group on the real pick path (fresh = constant; ×3 = 0.45; `reset()`), an exact scene line, and 4 mutants.
- **Canon:** sidecar `syn:Growth_3`.

**G1.6 CL-97 Thornsoil.**
- **Fix:** UM:1891 `thornsoilDPS = 6` becomes `max(thornsoilDPS, 6)`.
- **Coverage:** the "×7 Wildwood, then Thornsoil = 8" check fails at HEAD and passes after, so it proves the fix. 2 mutants.
- **Canon:** sidecar `v20_thornsoil`, `syn:Growth_7`.

**G1.7 `endSnowman` no longer cuts an Overload stun.**
- **Today:** `endSnowman` zeroes `stunTimer` (EN:623). The dazed stars hide only when `OverloadStunState`'s own timer ends (EN:879-881).
- **A snowman can be stunned:** neither `rollOverload` nor `applyOverloadStun` has a guard against it.
- **Sequence A:**
  - a 3s snowman takes a 1s Overload stun at 2.5s;
  - at 3.0s the enemy walks again with its stars up;
  - its immunity starts at 3.5s.
- **Sequence B:** the gun and the sweep roll Overload *before* `takeDamage` (GS:11808 → 11840; 12235 → 12253). So a T3 melt on an elite zeroes the 0.25/0.5s stun in the same hit: the proc is wasted and its immunity still starts.
- **Why it's determined:** it breaks the approved principle that one control "can't lengthen or cut short another" (closure table R2) and CL-2's stun durations.
- **Fix:**
  - `isStunned = stunTimer > 0 || snowman.isSnowman`;
  - `becomeSnowman` stops writing `stunTimer` (and drops its now-unused binding, for the zero-warnings gate);
  - `endSnowman` stops zeroing it.
  - Rec delivery: a pure `StunHold` in Systems with executed checks and 4 mutants.
- **Awareness:** a same-hit Overload on a melting elite will now hold it 0.25/0.5s after the melt.

**G1.8 CL-107 independent vulnerability channels** (decision: CL-116, Fracture).
- **Today:** one stored slot on `EnemyNode` (EN:111) and on the six `ArenaBossNode` conformers, applied in every `takeDamage` for every source (EN:959-961).
- **Writers:**
  - Frostbite (EN:884-885);
  - Called, re-written every frame (GS:6642-6648), and reset to 1.0 by `clearCalled` (GS:6960-6962);
  - Marked, only when the slot is 1.0 (GS:7000-7009);
  - **Fracture** (EN:660/882).
- **Seam:**
  - a pure `VulnerabilityChannels` (four terms, max);
  - the protocol requirement becomes the channel store, with a get-only `vulnerabilityMultiplier`, so all seven `takeDamage` lines stay byte-identical;
  - one line per boss conformer, and about 10 in GS.
- **Size:** about 65 production lines across 10 files, plus about 100 test lines (V1-V9, 6 mutants).
- **No card copy changes.** The sidecar updates for Apex, Polar Vortex and Skybeam.
- **Order:** must land before CL-94's vulnerability fold.

**G1.9 CL-94 unbiased rounding** (decisions: CL-114 shape, CL-115 consolidation).
- **Chains mapped end to end:**
  - gun → enemy (GS:11689-11924) has 11 truncation points, from the floor at 8179 to the in-node `.rounded()`;
  - gun → boss (11930-12006);
  - sweep → enemy (12191-12291);
  - sweep → boss (12296-12317).
- **The ruled amplifiers truncate one at a time:** Permafrost at 11759-11761, Brittle Cold at 11764-11766, Open Wounds at 11771-11773, and the sweep's copies at 12219-12227.
- **Marked and Called are `.rounded()` inside the node;** Overcharge is floored inside the base.
- **Nothing executes this arithmetic today.** The void harness executes only `VoidRounding`/`A6Rounding`; the rest is string pins.
- **Build plan once CL-114 is ruled, as three seams:**
  - **94a:** the pure routine, plus Permafrost/Brittle Cold/Open Wounds on all four chains, plus Permafrost's copy.
  - **94b:** the vulnerability fold (after G1.8).
  - **94c:** the Overcharge split.
- **Size:** about 250-400 lines including tests.
- **Executed checks:**
  - DH1: each amplifier moves a 1-damage hit;
  - DH2: the mean equals the exact product;
  - DH3: one rounding, not one per step;
  - DH4: minimum 1;
  - DH5: the integer prefix is unchanged;
  - DH6: identity at 1;
  - DH7: an independent threshold is unbiased (a reused one is caught as biased);
  - DH8: "never below today" holds on a grid.
- **Mutants:** 10.
- **Canon:** the Permafrost face and detail change (G2), so the canon regenerates. About 9 sidecar entries refresh.

**G1.10 CL-110 icicle crit** (decision: CL-117, premise correction).
- **Today:** `fireIcicle` hard-codes `isCrit: false` (GS:7677), so after T4 the gun never crits. Gouge and Hemorrhage then work only through Red Smile.
- **Fix:** roll the crit inside `fireIcicle`, which keeps void WR2's exact call-string pin. About 4-8 lines.
- **Numbers:** a crit icicle at base deals 4; with Hemorrhage, 6; Lucky Break takes those to 6 / 9. It crits on the boss too, and draws 1.5× large with the same hitbox.
- **No copy change.** No card says the icicle can't crit. The Gouge, Hemorrhage and Polar Vortex sidecar entries update.
- **Coverage:** string checks with a positive anchor (`isIcicle: true`); 2-3 mutants.

**G1.11 CL-99 Shatter** (decision: CL-118).
- **Ruled core:** mini-bosses take the 20% max-HP elite chunk instead of dying, reusing the Anomaly chunk helper. The chance and threshold are unchanged.
- **The seam also carries the recommended CL-118 items:**
  - the gun's Shatter exit charges the meters once;
  - it keeps its Iceburst shard generation;
  - the tuning moves to `GameConfig`.
- **No executed Shatter check exists today.**
- **Proposed coverage:** a pure `ShatterRule` in the chill harness plus 12 mutants.
- **Pins that must move:** catalog CP4 (it pins the old copy). Void FX10/WR11 count the Shatter exit's black-hole opening, so the restructure keeps it.

**G1.12 Hittability for secondary targeting** (A4c Q6; scope: CL-127a; gun auto-aim: CL-126).
- **Today:** `chainLightning`'s candidate loop (GS:12586) filters only `!isDying`, so a chain from the gun (11917), Electro Pulse (8373) or a Red Smile swing (12170) can land on a phased PaneStalker.
- **The same gap exists in:**
  - the Electro Pulse target (8358);
  - Sentry coil shocks (8446), whose boss fallback (8459) can shock the vanished Faceted Lie;
  - Relay Burn arcs (5221), which don't even filter dying bodies.
- **Fix:** skip non-hittable bodies and keep searching.
- **Ordering:** land this **before** CL-109's line-of-sight term, which goes into the same candidate filter.

**G1.13 CL-109 repairs that need no further ruling** (the semantics questions are CL-123/124).
- **Skybeam T3 homing** returns the lassoed prey before the line-of-sight loop that gates every other target (GS:5551-5554 vs 5563-5588). Give it the same predicate. The geometry 1A return said this was built; it wasn't.
- **The Tree T5 lion** steps with no resolve (GS:4417-4455). Resolve it after each step.
- **Wildbloom flowers** sample with no geometry check (GS:4568-4575). Rejection-sample off the Carrier; the zone centre is the always-valid fallback.
- **Vine Wall's shove** runs after the frame's geometry resolve (GS:4781 vs 5092). Move it ahead, beside the void-well pull, per the CL-77 precedent.
- **The deer's legacy `applyKnockback`** (GS:4005 → EN:993) gets the Guard path test (GS:6259-6282).
- **Scope and gate:** every CL-109 effect is reachable only in the Arena 6 campaign (plus the DEBUG shell), because the gauntlet has no Marchwarden case. Every fix is device-gated in Arena 6.
- **Coverage:** no harness compiles `ArenaGeometry.swift` today. A new `tools/geometry-harness` (G3.8) is the executed base for every geometry mutant.

**G1.14 Boss-hazard reach uses the live (Harden-shrunk) radius** (CL-59 + the A5 canon "the shrunk body is the one body"; scope: CL-127d).
- **Sites using the constant `GameConfig.Player.collisionRadius`:**
  - Quench Warden lanes (`QuenchWardenNode.swift:411`);
  - Dynamo Choir litany (`DynamoChoirNode.swift:465`);
  - Faceted Lie plates and Pane burst (`FacetedLieNode.swift:465, 616`);
  - the field-impulse wall clamp (GS:4861).
- **Already live:** the Grounder pulse, Relay Imp arcs, physics contact, and the monument/Carrier resolves.
- **Fix:** the three bosses get a settable `playerHitRadius`, written before `boss?.update`. About 15-20 lines across 4 files.
- **Effect:** reach shrinks 3-4pt with Harden (lanes 25 → 22, plates 40 → 37).

## Group 2 — copy/runtime reconciliation already ruled (exact strings for approval)

Per the CL-101 precedent, the exact strings come back **before** any copy changes. Each ships in the same seam as its behaviour.

**Fit rules:**
- **Faces:** greedy 17 characters per line, at most 3 lines with a detail and 4 without.
- **Detail modal:** in-run height at most 665pt on an iPhone SE. The model reproduces A7a's 665/652/647 exactly.
- **Chill and Shock synergy lines:** held to **1 line of 40** as well as 3×17. Polar Vortex and Skybeam are already at 665pt, and every card's modal lists its tree's synergies.

## Group 3 — test / debug / dormant cleanup (and the tooling the seams need)

**G3.1 Dormant runtime deletion** (scope: CL-127b).
- **Proven dormant against source:**
  - `arcWake*`, `inductionStep*` (the whole Induction machinery) and `bloodPriceBonus` are only ever 0/false, so their guards always return;
  - `shockChainRadiusBonus` is always `+ 0`;
  - `stunChance` is 0, so the `rollOverload` guard returns;
  - `unbrokenCoreOwned`, `glassEngineActive` and `staticCrownDamage`/`Radius` are written and never read.
- **Not involved:** no GameConfig constants and no UserDefaults keys.
- **Do NOT touch `UpgradeManager.retiredCardIDs`** (UM:1147-1156). CL-102's live-save registry depends on those strings, and they're in the catalog dump.
- **Size:** about −146 production lines (GS ≈ −89, PS ≈ −55, UM −2).
- **Only the guard harness breaks:** UB11, CA17 and CA18 get re-pointed to `projectileShield.isOwned`, the only grant site.
- **Canon:** only three sidecar prose fields change (Glass Engine, Static Crown, `syn:Guard_7`), because the deleted symbols aren't in the dump.
- **Scope rec (CL-127b):** also delete `stunDuration` and the legacy `else { enemy.applyStun(playerStats.stunDuration) }` branch (GS:12562-12564). Keep `EnemyNode.applyStun`.
- **Run it first:** two of its sites sit inside code other seams edit (the Induction block in `handleProjectileHit`, and GS:12585 in `chainLightning`).

**G3.2 Debug seams announce themselves** (the debug-seams rule).
- **`debugForcedCardID`** (UM:212, used at 676-686; `#if DEBUG`) has no badge. Add a Menlo-Bold 9 `⚠︎ DEBUG — forced card: <id>` label in `setupHUD` at +66, following the existing DEBUG banners at +30/+42/+54. About 14 lines, 1 exact-line check, 1 mutant.
- **Found alongside (CL-127c):** `GameConfig.Mote.debugForceEntrance` (GC:970, read at GS:9758) is compiled into release with no banner. It's cold today.

**G3.3 The A6 ledger's `liveMax`.**
- **Today:** GS:7856 logs `voidWells.count`. That count includes holes that expired this frame or are collapse-pending; they're only ended at GS:8023-8025.
- **Fix:** pass the live count. One DEBUG line plus a void WR check.
- **Suite limit:** the retained suite can't mutate it until `mutations.py` copies `Sparkforge/Utils` (G3.8).

**G3.4 Scene-boundary test hardening (the carried A4c and A5 items). Zero production change.**
- **A4c meters.**
  - **Gap:** MC1-MC4 execute only `RedSmileContact`, and the redsmile harness never reads GameScene. Deleting `chargeRedSmileHitMeters(.shatter)`, the exact A4c F2 bug, passes every check today.
  - **Add a WR group:**
    - exactly 3 call sites;

## Execution order (proposed)

Each seam runs the established loop: build → own mutation suite (every behaviour fixed gets a retained mutant that is **KILLED**) → freeze with a SHA-256 manifest → independent Reviewer → correctives with a snapshot and a corrective diff → Brandon's gate. A seam that changes catalog copy or sidecar prose regenerates the canon and passes `--check`. The unit closes with **one commit**, staged file by file, pushed with approval, followed by the Atlas republish.

**Dependencies that fix the order:**
- The dormant deletion comes first, because it shrinks lines that CL-94 and the chain seams edit.
- CL-107 before CL-94b and before Erasure's CL-119 A1.
- Hittability before CL-109's arc test (same predicate).
- The CP4 copy pins bundle CL-94/96/99, so their copy lands in order.
- The tooling (G3.8) comes before any seam whose mutants live in `Nodes/`, `Utils/` or `ArenaGeometry`.

| Seam | Content | Needs | Production diff | Tests | Gate | Canon |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **S0a** | Tooling: `COPY_DIRS` += Nodes, Utils; retire the 4 Stubs mirrors → extract; comment stripping in the guard harness | — | 0 | ~60 | static | — |
| **S0b** | `tools/geometry-harness` baseline + the modal-ceiling and 1×40 synergy guards | — | 0 | ~200 | static | — |
| **S1** | G3.1 dormant deletion (+ CL-127b) | CL-127b | ≈ −146 | 3 re-pointed | static (zero new warnings; all green) | sidecar prose |
| **S2** | G3.2 debug banners (+ CL-127c) · G3.3 `liveMax` · G3.4 scene-boundary hardening | CL-127c | ~20 (DEBUG) | ~80, ~16 mutants | static + sim screenshot | — |
| **S3** | G1.1 Apex on the gun's boss path · G1.2 Grounder/Relay · G1.3 Storm Engine · G1.4 Erasure band | — | ~15 | ~90 | device | sidecar |
| **S4** | G1.5 Rootbound · G1.6 Thornsoil | — | ~5 | ~35 | device | sidecar |
| **S5** | G1.7 snowman/Overload stun hold | — | ~30 | ~60 | static + device note | — |
| **S6** | G1.8 CL-107 channels (+ CL-116) · G2-5 Apex T4 face | CL-116, G2-5 | ~65 / 10 files | ~100 | device | copy + sidecar |
| **S7** | G1.9 94a: the pure routine on all 4 chains · Permafrost/Brittle/Open Wounds · G2-1 Permafrost copy | CL-114, CL-115, G2-1 | ~120 | ~150 | device | copy + sidecar |
| **S8** | G1.9 94b the vulnerability fold · 94c Overcharge | S6, S7 | ~100 | ~100 | device | sidecar |

**If fewer cycles are wanted:**
- The static seams (S0a, S0b, S1, S2) can share one Reviewer pass and one static gate.
- Brandon's device gates can batch by phase: S3-S5 · S6-S10 · S11 · S12-S15, with each seam still frozen and independently reviewed on its own.
- The order doesn't change either way.

## What Brandon / the Command Center were asked for — ✅ answered Sep 28

All four asks are answered in §B7's FINAL RULINGS: the rows ruled; Group 2 approved as proposed; the A7a doc cleanup named for S16; the order approved minus S11b, with Reviewer/device batching. Next: the docs-only rulings baseline commit, then S0a.

## Build — the static batch S0a · S0b · S1 · S2 (Sep 28)

The rulings baseline is `625d28e` (docs only, not pushed). The four static seams were built one at a time, each with its own retained mutants, then validated together. Per the approved batching, they share **one Reviewer pass and one static gate**. Per-seam diffs (session scratchpad `a7b/`): `s0a.patch` (262 lines), `s0b.patch` (346), `s1.patch` (504), `s2.patch` (242). Retained mutants are prefixed `B7-`: `python3 tools/catalog-harness/mutations.py B7` runs all of A7b's, and `B7-S1` runs one seam's.

**S0a — tooling (zero production change).**
- `mutations.py` `COPY_DIRS` now also copies `Sparkforge/Nodes` and `Sparkforge/Utils`, so the void and hud harnesses can be named as checks and `EnemyNode`/boss/`CombatLedger` mutants can run.
- `Stubs.swift` no longer hand-mirrors `GameConfig.Growth`, `.Chill`, `.BossClass` or `.Erasure`. The 10 shared-stub harnesses extract the real blocks (`extract-config.sh … Growth Chill BossClass Erasure`). Every mirrored value was checked identical to the real one first. The real blocks depend only on `DeviceScale` (stubbed to 1).
- The guard harness reads the scene as code, with `//` comments stripped, for every wiring check, and **WR0b** proves the stripping is in force.
- Mutants **B7-S0a-1…4: 4/4 KILLED.**
- **Negative controls** (the same mutants run on the pre-S0a tree, `git archive 625d28e`):
  - S0a-2 (the real Permafrost value changed) **SURVIVED**, because the chill harness read the stub mirror;
  - the suite couldn't even baseline the void harness (**WR0**: `Nodes/` wasn't copied);
  - S0a-1 had nothing to match.

**S0b — tooling (zero production change).**
- **`tools/geometry-harness`** (the 12th harness) compiles the real `ArenaGeometry`, `MeleeSector` and `VectorMath` plus the real `GameConfig.Geometry`. The Splitworks `radiusScale` (1.15) and the phone arena radius (350) are parsed from source, not mirrored (**GR0**). Seeded checks:
  - **G0:** an open arena blocks, resolves and occludes nothing;
  - **G1:** the Splitworks is exactly one Fallen Carrier, solid at its centre, with both anchors clear;
  - **G2:** resolve ends 5.95/10/14/26.4pt actors clear and leaves valid points untouched;
  - **G3:** the exact segment test equals a 0.25pt brute-force march on 20,000 random segments;
  - **G4:** the stepped test never over-reports, and it misses short corner-clipping segments (132 of 20,000; the shortest 64.8pt), which is why CL-109 uses the exact test;
  - **G5:** the shared sampler never places inside the Carrier.
  - Informational: the shortest blocked segment between two 14pt-clear points measures ~62pt, which supports CL-123(c) (a 35pt burst can't cross the Carrier).
- **Catalog MD0–MD2, the detail-modal guard.**
  - `CardDetailNode` is SpriteKit and isn't compiled on the host, so its vertical layout is modelled. **MD0** pins every spacing, wrap width, pad, the wrap rule and the content builder to the node's own code lines.
  - **MD1:** every card is ≤ 667pt (the iPhone SE scene, in-run).
  - **MD2:** above A7a's 665pt target sits exactly {Phase 666}, anchored by Polar Vortex = 665.
  - The model reproduces A7a's numbers exactly. It **subsumes the Chill/Shock 1×40 synergy rule**: a synergy line that grows pushes its tree's tallest card past 665 (B7-S0b-4 proves it).
- Mutants **B7-S0b-1…5: 5/5 KILLED.**

**S1 — the dormant runtime deletion (G3.1 + CL-127b).**
- **Deleted**, each proven dead at `625d28e`:
  - `arcWake*` (PS fields, the scene state, `updateArcWake`, `spawnArcWakeVisual`, the update call, the restart clear);
  - the whole Induction Step machinery (PS fields and functions; the scene's movement charge and its now-unused `preMovePosition`; the hit-chain discharge block);
  - `bloodPriceBonus` (and its branch in `effectiveDamageMultiplier`);
  - `unbrokenCoreOwned`;
  - `staticCrownDamage` and `staticCrownRadius`;
  - `shockChainRadiusBonus` (the chain range is now `GameConfig.Shock.chainRange`);
  - `stunChance` and `stunDuration`, plus `rollOverload`'s legacy `applyStun` branch (`effectiveStunChance` returns 0 without Overload);
  - `glassEngineActive`.
- **Kept:** `EnemyNode.applyStun`, and `UpgradeManager.retiredCardIDs` (live-save Codex data).
- Production: 3 files, about −150 lines. A stale "blood price" doc comment was also fixed.
- Guard UB11/CA17/CA18 now read `projectileShield.isOwned`, the only grant site.
- Three sidecar sensitivities lost their dormant-field clauses, so the canon was regenerated; `--check` is valid.
- **Catalog WR7:** none of the deleted tokens may return in code, with positive anchors.
- Mutants **B7-S1-1…2: KILLED** (shock O6 + WR7; WR7).

**S2 — debug seams, the ledger, and the carried scene-boundary hardening.**
- **Banners:** `setupHUD` (DEBUG) badges `UpgradeManager.debugForcedCardID` at +66 (`⚠︎ DEBUG — forced card: <id>`) and `GameConfig.Mote.debugForceEntrance` at +78 (`⚠︎ DEBUG — Mote entrance forced`).
- **The Mote flag** now sits inside `#if DEBUG`. `tryMoteEntrance` is restructured (a `let` with `#if DEBUG`/`#else`), so Release compiles no flag and no new "never mutated" warning (CL-127c).
- **The ledger:** `recordVoidWell` logs the **live** hole count.
- **redsmile MW1–MW6** (the carried A4c item; the scene is now read):
  - exactly three contact sites;
  - every `return` in `redSmileHit` follows its charge (F2);
  - the boss contact ends with its charge (F1);
  - no direct meter call in a Red Smile hit;
  - the helper hands `RedSmileContact` the meters' own methods;
  - the gun's enemy hit registers each meter once, and its boss hit registers Erasure once (S3 adds Apex).
- **guard WR18** (the carried A5 item): every Repulse launch, shove and pin call and both Ironhide calls are matched whole, whitespace-normalised and counted once, with each body's own flags.
- **void WR29:** the live-hole ledger. **catalog WR8:** the banners and the Mote guard.
- Mutants **B7-S2-1…10: 10/10 KILLED**. They replay A4c F1 and F2, the carried A5 `isSnowman: false` miswiring, the Ironhide snowman flag, a hidden banner, the Mote flag leaking into Release, and `liveMax` counting ended holes.
- **Sim proof (static):**
  - An isolated copy with both flags hot (AudioManager stubbed) shows both banners in the run HUD at 60fps (`a7b/s2-debug-banners.png`).
  - The clean build was then reinstalled and verified: no banners.

**Validation (the whole batch):**

After corrective 1, the full suite gives **232/232 KILLED** (A7a's 199 + A7b's 33). The unmutated baseline passes every check the suite names, the new geometry harness included.

## Internal review of the static batch → corrective 1 (Sep 28, before freeze 1)

A read-only internal reviewer attacked the batch through the per-seam diffs, running its own mutants in scratch copies.

**Verdict: no production defect.**
- **S1 is behaviour-neutral.** Every deleted field was only ever set to its default, or written and read only by tests; `rollOverload` is equivalent, with RNG consumption unchanged; no UserDefaults key or config constant was touched.
- **S2 is release-safe.** Both flags are DEBUG-only, and Release compiles `let eligible = earned`.
- **The S0a extraction is value-identical**, field by field.
- G3's ground truth is sound (0.125pt worst-case march error, inside the 0.2pt skip band), and WR18 and the MD model match the code.

**What it did find:** 2 MED and 5 LOW test gaps, each backed by a surviving mutant (R1–R11). All were fixed in **corrective 1** (harness and docs only; `a7b/corrective1.patch`, 376 lines, this packet included). Every survivor is now a retained mutant, and all are KILLED.

| Finding | Fix | Retained mutant(s) |
|---|---|---|
| **MED-1** MW2/MW3 counted only lines *starting* with `return`, so a one-line `…; return nil` replayed A4c F2 | Count every `return` token (exactly 2 in the enemy hit, 0 in the boss hit) | B7-S2-11/12/13 (R1–R3) |
| **MED-2** MD0 pinned the spacing tokens but not the layout's structure, start or panel formula (a `y -= 3` moved into the rung loop pushed Polar Vortex to 677pt and passed) | MD0 also pins the layout skeleton: every loop/branch, where each spacing sits, `var y: CGFloat = 0`, `let contentHeight = -y`, the `panelH` formula | B7-S0b-6/7/8 (R4–R6) |
| **LOW-3** G5 never reached the sampler's give-up fallback; the header overclaimed seeding | **G5b:** an annulus deep inside the Carrier, so every candidate is rejected and the fallback must resolve out; header corrected | B7-S0b-9 (R7) |
| **LOW-4** WR29 didn't pin the ledger call after the append | Order check: append, then record | B7-S2-14 (R8) |
| **LOW-5** WR8 didn't prove the banners sit inside `#if DEBUG` (a Release compile error was the only guard) | `insideDebug` for both banners and the Mote read | B7-S2-16 (R11) |
| **LOW-6** WR7's tokens were case-sensitive (`updateArcWake` slipped through) | Add `ArcWake`/`InductionStep`; `rollOverload` has no `applyStun(` (and does call `applyOverloadStun(`) | B7-S1-3 (R9), B7-S1-4 |
| **LOW-7** MW6 was count-only (`if !enemyNode.isDying { apexRegisterAttack() }` passed); stale tool docs | Each registration is its own unconditional line; the docs fixed (`mutations.py` docstring, the redsmile header and `run.sh`, the catalog README MD row, the geometry header) | B7-S2-15 (R10) |

Also taken from the review's informational notes: **GR0** now pins the stub's radius formula to GameConfig's own line (`DeviceScale.arenaRadius * ArenaConfig.current.radiusScale`).

**Noted, not changed (out of S0a's ruled scope; banked for the closeout audit):**
- `Stubs.swift` still hand-mirrors 13 other config blocks. All are value-equal on a phone except Skybeam's `acquireRangeT1/T2`, which is a flat 200/280 in the stub but a fraction × arena radius in the app.
- The MD model covers the in-run detail builder. The Codex hand-builds its detail content (`CardCodexNode.swift:247-266`), which is the shorter case (no tier line) and unpinned.
- `rollOverload`'s inner `if playerStats.overloadOwned` is now always true when reached. Flattening it is optional and was left alone (minimal diff).
- guard `run.sh`'s unread `GUARD_CONFIG` (already noted in recon).

A snapshot of the frozen files is in `a7b/freeze1-snap/`. The digest is the first 16 hex of the manifest's own SHA-256.

  1. **S1 is behaviour-neutral.** Every deleted symbol was dead at `625d28e`. `rollOverload`'s result and RNG consumption are unchanged. No persisted key is touched. `retiredCardIDs` and `EnemyNode.applyStun` stay.
  2. **S2 is release-safe.** Every new seam is DEBUG-only. Release compiles no Mote flag and gains no warning. The debug-seams rule is met: each hot flag shows a HUD badge.

The house rule applies: executed where possible; string checks match code only, with positive anchors. Mutation-test them; the retained `B7-*` mutants are the floor, not the ceiling.

  5. **The MD model is CardDetailNode's layout.** MD1/MD2 are then true statements about the iPhone SE.

## Independent review of freeze 1: FAIL (test strength only) → corrective 2 (Sep 28)

**The review:** freeze 1 (`0a481fdacaa4c31f`) was reviewed exactly. **No gameplay or production defect.** It found five independent surviving mutations. The corrective-2 scope is recorded as given:

> 1. Block comments must not satisfy source assertions. The Red Smile and Guard source readers currently strip only `//`. Replace that with lexical source sanitization sufficient for the Swift files under test: remove line comments; remove block comments; handle nested Swift block comments; do not treat comment markers inside string literals as comments. Apply the sanitized source to the relevant structural assertions. Retain both Reviewer reproductions (actual Red Smile meter call commented out with `/* ... */`; correct Repulse call preserved only in a block comment while live code uses `isSnowman: false`). Both must fail. Do not solve these with regexes targeted only to the exact mutations.
> 2. MW6 must prove unconditional registration … not nested behind an additional `if`, `guard` success branch, loop, closure, or other conditional wrapper. A multiline `if !enemyNode.isDying { apexRegisterAttack() }` must fail. Preserve the existing single-registration/order expectations. Prefer a small brace-depth / structural source check over adding gameplay production code.
> 3. Geometry config must be authoritative. GR0 must not execute a hand-copied arena-radius formula … A production formula change with the old formula retained only in a comment must fail because the executed value changed … Confirm no second radius formula remains independently maintained in the geometry harness.
> 4. WR8 must prove hot-flag reachability … The Reviewer's `if false { <forced-card banner> }` reproduction must fail. Prefer a structural branch-depth check …
>
> Add all five independent Review reproductions as retained mutants. Keep the existing 33 A7b mutants and 199 A7a mutants. … Cut freeze 2 only after every new reproduction is killed, then stop for independent re-review. Do not begin S3.

**Before any fix:** freeze 1 was verified (27/27 files, digest `0a481fdacaa4c31f`) and snapshotted (`a7b/snap-pre-c2`). The five reproductions were written as retained mutants **B7-C2-1…5** and run against freeze 1: **all five SURVIVED**, so each fix is proven against a real survivor.

**What changed** (tools only; `a7b/corrective2.patch`, 668 lines, 14 files):

1. **One shared lexical sanitizer**, `tools/signature-draw-harness/SwiftSource.swift`.
   - A small Swift lexer, not a targeted regex. It removes line comments, block comments and **nested** block comments, keeping newlines so line structure survives.
   - String literals are left intact: plain, escaped, `\( … )` interpolation (which can itself hold strings), multi-line `"""` and raw `#"…"#`. A `//` or `/*` inside a string is not a comment.
   - It also gives a `shape` view: the same text, character for character, with string contents blanked, used for brace structure.
   - It is applied to **every** harness that reads source instead of compiling it: **guard, redsmile, catalog and void**. The review named the first two; catalog and void had the identical `//`-only reader, so fixing only two would have left the same hole open. All four harnesses passed unchanged on the new reader, so no existing check had relied on the old line-cut.
   - Guard **WR0b** now proves the scene is read through it: the raw text differs, and sanitising again changes nothing.
   - **Catalog SX1–SX4** execute the sanitizer itself on fixtures: every comment form removed; every string form kept; the shape view aligned; and `Block`'s depth, enclosing block and early-exit count, with braces inside strings and comments ignored.
2. **`SwiftSource.Block`**, a structural view of a function body: occurrences, brace depth, the directly enclosing block, the matching brace, and the `return`s before a point.
   - **redsmile MW7**: each gun meter registration sits **directly in the hit function's body** (depth 1: not in an `if`, a guard-else, a loop or a closure). It also comes after exactly the intended early exits:
     - the enemy hit's 5: the boss route, the node guard, Glass Blood, the Void secondaries and Shatter;
     - the boss hit's 3: the node guard, Glass Blood and the Void secondaries.
   - Apex comes before Erasure. MW6 (single registration, exact lines) is unchanged.
3. **The geometry harness executes the production radius formula.**
   - `GameConfig.Arena` is now **extracted** like every other config block, so `radius { DeviceScale.arenaRadius * ArenaConfig.current.radiusScale }` runs as shipped.
   - The stub supplies only the two inputs, both parsed from the app's source.
   - **GR1** pins the **executed** Splitworks radius on a phone (402.5pt), so a formula or tuning change fails on the value; a deliberate retune updates the pin.
   - The corrective-1 source-string pin and `GEO_CONFIG` are gone. **No radius formula remains in the geometry harness** outside comments (checked by grep).
4. **Catalog WR8 proves hot-flag reachability.**
   - Each flag's `if` sits directly in `setupHUD`'s body after only its opening guard.
   - The banner's `seam.text` and its `camera.addChild(seam)` sit directly inside **that** branch's own block.
   - The `#if DEBUG` placement check is kept.

**Retained coverage:**
- **B7-C2-1…5**, the Reviewer's five reproductions: **all KILLED**.
  - C2-1 Red Smile `/* … */` → MW1, MW2.
  - C2-2 the Repulse pin in a block comment with live `isSnowman: false` → WR10, WR18.
  - C2-3 the multi-line `if !enemyNode.isDying { … }` → MW7.
  - C2-4 a formula change with the old formula in a comment → GR1.
  - C2-5 the forced-card banner under `if false { … }` → WR8.
- **Additional variants:**
  - **C2-6…9** mutate the sanitizer itself: nested comments end early; strings not recognised; escapes ignored; `Block.depth` ignores nesting. All are KILLED by SX1–SX4.
  - **C2-10** wraps the whole forced-card branch in `if false`, and **C2-11** inserts a new `guard !enemyNode.isDying else { return }` before The Hunter's registration. Both are KILLED.
- **B7-S0a-1** now mutates the new reader (`let src = raw`) and is still KILLED.
- **Total: A7b 44 mutants, all KILLED.** The existing 33 are kept, with S0a-1 re-pointed.

**Validation (corrective 2):**
- **Harnesses: 812/812 across twelve.** bleed 115 · catalog 94 (+4: SX1–SX4) · chill 43 · damage-pipeline 53 · fire 53 · geometry 9 (+1: GR1) · guard 109 · hud 16 · redsmile 66 (+1: MW7) · shock 32 · signature-draw 22 · void 200.
- **Canon:** `--check` valid; the fingerprint is unchanged (`sha256:3839e803…`).
- **Builds:** **clean** Debug and **clean** Release builds both succeed. Warnings appear only in the four pre-existing files.
- **Production:** byte-identical to freeze 1. All four production files verify against the freeze-1 manifest, and `Sparkforge/` differs only by an untracked Finder `.DS_Store`. No DEBUG-only testability change was needed.
- **Phase:** MD1/MD2 hold. Every card is ≤ 667pt, and Phase at 666pt is still the only card above the 665pt copy target.
- **The banked notes stay banked, not expanded:** the other 13 config mirrors (Skybeam's lasso-reach discrepancy included), and the canon generator's `--help` behaviour.
- **Retained mutation suite:** `python3 tools/catalog-harness/mutations.py -j 8` gives **243/243 KILLED** (A7a's 199 + A7b's 44). Its baseline passes every check it names.

## Independent review of freeze 2: FAIL (two test-strength blockers) → corrective 3 (Sep 28)

**The review:** freeze 2 (`c8acf419e3ce8909`) was reviewed exactly. **No production defect.** It found two blockers, recorded as given:

> Corrective 3 governing principle. Extend `SwiftSource` from a comment-cleaned view into a lightweight executable-code structural view. Do not patch the four exact strings individually. The structural view used by MW7 and WR8 must exclude source text that cannot execute: comments; string-literal contents, including multiline/raw/interpolated forms already supported by the lexer; inactive conditional-compilation regions relevant to the source under test, including the Reviewer's `#if false` reproductions. Preserve line structure / brace structure sufficiently for the existing depth checks. Do not alter production game code.
> 1. MW7, executable registration only … These must fail: the call exists only as text inside a multiline string; the call exists only inside an inactive `#if false` region; all previously retained conditional / guard / loop / closure wrapper cases. Search and structural depth analysis must operate on the executable-code view …
> 2. WR8, hot branch must actually reach the banner. WR8 must prove: the intended hot-flag branch itself is active/executable; the banner creation is executable inside that branch; there is no unconditional function exit between entering the hot branch and reaching the banner. … Do not merely count returns before the branch. Inspect the path inside the branch up to the banner. A structural check for unconditional exits in that prefix is sufficient for this scope. Do not build a general Swift control-flow engine.
> 3. Lexer / structural fixtures … 4. Retained mutants: MW7 multiline-string fake registration; MW7 `#if false` registration; WR8 `guard false else { return }` before banner; WR8 hot branch inside `#if false`. Also retain every existing A7b mutant. … Cut freeze 3 only after all four new reproductions are killed. Stop for independent re-review. Do not begin S3.
>
> *(Brandon:)* Don't build a mini Swift compiler. We need just enough token awareness to distinguish code from comments / strings / inactive compile regions, and then enough local control-flow awareness to answer: "Can execution reach this line from this branch without an unconditional exit?"

**Before any fix:**
- Freeze 2 was verified (28/28, digest `c8acf419e3ce8909`) and snapshotted (`a7b/snap-pre-c3`).
- The four reproductions were written as **B7-C3-1…4** and run against freeze 2, and **all four SURVIVED**.
- **A note on faithfulness.** My first WR8 forms put the `guard false` and the `#if false` in the *forced-card* branch. Freeze 2 already killed them, but only **incidentally**: the inserted `return` shifted the Mote banner's pinned exit count, and the added `#endif` broke the Mote banner's DEBUG-placement check. The forced-card branch check itself would have passed. So the retained reproductions (C3-3, C3-4) target the **Mote** banner, the last in the DEBUG block, where nothing downstream catches them. The forced-card forms are kept as C3-5 and C3-6, which the new logic now kills directly.

**What changed** (tools only, no game code; `a7b/corrective3.patch`, 385 lines, 4 files):

1. **`SwiftSource.executable`: token awareness, not a compiler.**
   - It is the shape view (comments removed, string contents blanked) with every **inactive conditional-compilation region**, and every `#if`/`#elseif`/`#else`/`#endif` line, blanked too. It stays character-aligned with the code and shape views.
   - Conditions are evaluated under `activeFlags = ["DEBUG"]`, the app's DEBUG build that the harnesses prove. That covers `DEBUG`, `true`/`false`, `!`, `&&`, `||` and parentheses, with nesting and `#elseif`; anything else is false.
   - That is exactly enough: **the app's only directive today is `#if DEBUG`** (122 uses; checked by grep).
   - Directives are read on the string-blanked view, so a `#if` inside a string literal is never a directive.
2. **`Block` now answers every structural question on the executable view** (block bounds, depth, enclosing block, matching brace, returns). It adds:
   - **`executableOffsets(of:)`:** a match must begin in code, not in a string, and none of its code characters may lie in an inactive region.
   - **`exitFree(from:to:)`:** the local path check. The executable code in the range holds **no exit statement** (`return`, `throw`, `break`, `continue`, `fatalError`, `preconditionFailure`) and **no `guard`**. It is a straight path, so the target runs whenever the start is reached.
   - This is deliberately stricter than "no *unconditional* exit": the banner branch is straight-line code, and any exit in its prefix would make the banner conditional or unreachable. It is not a control-flow engine.
3. **MW7** searches only executable occurrences, then applies the existing depth-1 and intended-exit-count rules (the enemy hit 5, the boss hit 3) on the executable view.
4. **WR8** requires all of the following:
   - the hot-flag `if` is itself **executable**, directly in `setupHUD`'s body after only its opening guard;
   - the banner text and its `addChild` are executable and sit directly in **that** branch's block;
   - the path inside the branch, from its `{` to the `addChild`, is **exit-free**;
   - the `#if DEBUG` placement check (kept).
5. **Fixtures SX5–SX8** (catalog):
   - **SX5:** call-looking text in plain, escaped, interpolated, multi-line and raw strings is not executable, while a live call is.
   - **SX6:** inactive content is not executable, and active DEBUG content is. It covers `#if false`, `#else`, `#if !DEBUG … #elseif true`, a nested `#if false` inside `#if DEBUG`, and `DEBUG && false` / `DEBUG || false`.
   - **SX7:** braces and comment markers inside plain and multi-line strings never corrupt depth.
   - **SX8:** an exit, or a `guard` whose else ends in an unlisted `Never` call, breaks the path, and a straight path holds.

**Retained coverage:**
- **B7-C3-1…4**, the Reviewer's four reproductions: **all KILLED** (MW7, MW7, WR8, WR8).
- **Additional:**
  - C3-5/6: the forced-card forms, now killed by WR8 directly.
  - C3-7: inactive regions stay executable → SX6.
  - C3-8: a string match counts as executable → SX5.
  - C3-9: the path check ignores `guard` → SX8.
- **B7-C2-9** was re-pointed at the executable view, since `depth` moved there, and is still KILLED.
- **Every existing A7b mutant is retained. A7b now has 53 in total.**

**Validation (corrective 3):**
- **Harnesses: 816/816 across twelve** (catalog 98: +4, SX5–SX8; the rest unchanged).
- **Canon:** `--check` valid; the fingerprint is unchanged.
- **Builds:** **clean** Debug and **clean** Release both succeed. Warnings appear only in the four pre-existing files.
- **Production:** the four gameplay files verify against the freeze-2 manifest, and `Sparkforge/` is byte-identical to freeze 2.
- **Phase:** MD1/MD2 hold. Phase at 666pt is still the sole card above 665, and every card is ≤ 667.
- **The banked notes stay out of this corrective:** the config mirrors and the canon generator's `--help`.
- **Retained mutation suite:** `python3 tools/catalog-harness/mutations.py -j 8` gives **252/252 KILLED** (A7a's 199 + A7b's 53).

## Independent review of freeze 3: FAIL (two test-strength blockers) → corrective 4 (Sep 29)

**The review:** freeze 3 (`41be43fac278f7f2`) was reviewed exactly. **No production defect**; S3 stays blocked. The scope is recorded as given:

> Corrective 4 principle. Do not patch the exact `#if/**/false`, tab, or `fatalError` strings. Fix the two underlying structural-model weaknesses.
> 1. SwiftSource comment removal must preserve token boundaries. When removing comments, replace removed content with whitespace/newline-preserving blanks rather than concatenating adjacent source tokens. Therefore `#if/**/false` must normalize structurally to the equivalent of `#if false`, not `#iffalse`. Preserve line positions and brace structure needed by existing checks. Add fixtures proving token separation across block comments; nested block comments; comments between directive tokens; spaces; tabs; newlines where legal; ordinary executable tokens separated by comments. String contents remain excluded from executable-code analysis as already ruled.
> 2. Conditional-compilation handling must parse directive tokens. Stop recognizing inactive regions through selected textual prefixes. Parse `#if`, `#elseif`, `#else`, and `#endif` structurally after lexical cleanup, with arbitrary Swift whitespace/tabs between directive tokens. At minimum, correctly evaluate … literal `false`; literal `true`; `DEBUG` under the harness's intended DEBUG configuration; negation where encountered in the current source; nested inactive regions. If the current source uses more complex compile conditions, enumerate them first and support only the forms actually required. Do not build a general Swift conditional-expression evaluator unless source evidence requires one. Retain: `#if/**/false`; `#if<TAB>false`; the previous ordinary `#if false`; nested inactive-region variants. Both MW7 and WR8 must operate on this active executable-code view.
> 3. MW7 must validate control flow, not `return` count. … Establish the accepted top-level pre-registration control-flow skeleton for each gun-hit function from the frozen production baseline. Require: `apexRegisterAttack()` is an executable direct-body call at the accepted location; the ordered set/count of direct-body early-exit/control-flow statements before it matches the accepted baseline; no additional `guard`, `if`, loop, closure, switch, or equivalent control-flow wrapper may be introduced before registration without failing the assertion. The check must not care whether an added guard's terminating branch uses `return`, `fatalError`, `throw`, another noreturn expression … Retain the Reviewer's exact mutant `guard !enemyNode.isDying else { fatalError(...) }` and add at least one equivalent guard using a different terminator if practical. Do not change production merely to make the structural test easier.
> 4. WR8. No new hot-path control-flow defect was reported beyond conditional-compilation parsing. Preserve Freeze 3's existing branch-reachability assertions and rerun all prior WR8 mutants against the corrected active-code view. Specifically retain: Mote branch inside `#if/**/false`; tab-separated inactive directive; ordinary `#if false`; prior `guard false else { return }`; prior independent false wrapper.
> Validation … verify gameplay/catalog/canon inputs remain byte-identical to Freeze 3; preserve the documented Phase 666pt exception and ≤665pt rule for all others. Keep the App Store icon/workspace changes outside the frozen A7b set exactly as found. Do not absorb unrelated `Info.plist` formatting or workspace changes into this corrective. Cut Freeze 4 only after the new Review reproductions are killed. Stop for independent re-review. Do not begin S3.

**Before any fix:**
- Freeze 3 was verified (28/28, digest `41be43fac278f7f2`) and snapshotted (`a7b/snap-pre-c4`).
- **Unrelated work found in the tree, and left exactly as found:**
  - the App Store icon swap, **staged in the index** (three `App Store Icon*.png` deleted, three `Sparkforge New App Icon*.png` added, `AppIcon.appiconset/Contents.json` modified);
  - `Sparkforge/Info.plist`, modified and unstaged.
- **The freeze script no longer derives its file list from `git diff`,** which would have swept those in. It now reads a **pinned A7b file list** (`a7b/a7b-set.files`: freeze 3's 28 files; a corrective that adds a file adds it there).
- **Reproductions:** written as **B7-C4-1…9** and run against freeze 3.
  - **Six SURVIVED**, confirming both classes:
    - C4-1 and C4-2: MW7's `#if/**/false` and `#if<TAB>false`;
    - C4-4 and C4-5: MW7's `guard … else { fatalError(…) }` and `{ preconditionFailure() }`;
    - C4-6 and C4-7: WR8's Mote branch in `#if/**/false` and `#if<TAB>false`.
  - Three were already KILLED and are retained as regression variants: C4-3 (a nested inactive region), C4-8 (`#if !DEBUG`, negation) and C4-9 (an `if false` wrapper on the Mote banner).

**What changed** (tools only; `a7b/corrective4.patch`, 4 files plus the catalog README's SX row):

1. **Comments are blanked, not deleted.** Every removed character becomes a space, and newlines stay. So `#if/**/false` reads `#if     false` and `a/**/b` stays two tokens.
   - Every view (code, shape, executable) is now aligned character for character **with the raw source itself**, so line positions and brace structure are preserved exactly.
   - All 12 harnesses passed unchanged on the new reader. SX1–SX3 now state the blanked forms.
2. **Directives are parsed as tokens, after cleanup.** A directive is optional spaces or tabs, then `#` immediately followed by `if`/`elseif`/`else`/`endif`, then the condition. There's no textual prefix matching any more.
   - **Enumerated first:** the app's only directive is `#if DEBUG` (122 uses).
   - **Supported, exactly as the ruling names them:** `true`, `false`, a flag (`DEBUG`, active) and `!` negation (repeatable), with nesting, `#elseif` and `#else`.
   - **Anything else is refused, never guessed:** `unsupportedDirectives` flags it, and `block(in:after:)` returns nil, so the check fails loudly.
   - The corrective-3 `&&`/`||`/parenthesis evaluator is **removed**, because no source uses those forms.
3. **MW7 validates control flow, not a `return` count.** `Block.skeleton(before:)` is the body's **direct** control-flow skeleton, in order: every control keyword written directly in it (`if`, `guard`, `else`, `for`, `while`, `repeat`, `switch`, `case`, `default`, `do`, `catch`, `defer`, `return`, `throw`, `break`, `continue`) and every block opened directly in it (`{`: an if or guard body, a loop, a closure).
   - MW7 requires each registration to be an executable, depth-1 call whose skeleton before it **equals the accepted baseline**, taken from freeze 3's production code:
     - the **enemy hit**: the boss-route `if`, the node `guard … else`, then 20 direct `if` blocks (the Glass Blood, Void-secondary and Shatter exits among them);
     - the **boss hit**: the node `guard … else`, then 9 direct `if` blocks.
   - Any added guard changes the skeleton, whatever ends its else. So does any added `if`, loop, switch or closure. Production is unchanged.
4. **WR8:** freeze 3's branch-reachability assertions are **unchanged**. They now run on the corrected executable view.

**Fixtures (catalog):**
- **SX9:** tokens never fuse across block, nested block and line comments (the newline is kept), and `guard/**/ok` is still a `guard`.
- **SX10:** directives as tokens:
  - `#if/**/false`, `#if<TAB>false`, `#if  false`, a leading tab, `#if /* a */ false` and `#if !/**/DEBUG` are all inactive;
  - `#if<TAB>true`, `#if/*x*/DEBUG`, `#if !false` and `#if !!DEBUG` are all active;
  - a nested `#if/**/false` inside `#if DEBUG` is inactive.
- **SX11:** `#if os(iOS)` and `#if DEBUG && false` are flagged, and a block containing one is refused.
- **SX12:** the skeleton. A baseline body gives `if { guard else { {`. Each of these added forms changes it: a guard ending in `fatalError`, `preconditionFailure` or `throw`; an `if`; a `for`; a `while`; a `switch`; a trailing closure.
- **SX6** now proves negation and nesting in place of the removed `&&`/`||` forms.

**Retained coverage:**
- **B7-C4-1…9:** all KILLED, the six freeze-3 survivors included.
- **Mutants of the machinery itself:**
  - C4-10: comments deleted again → SX1/2/3/9.
  - C4-11: the directive parser skips spaces but not tabs → SX10.
  - C4-12: the skeleton ignores `guard` → SX9, SX12, MW7.
  - C4-13: unsupported directives accepted silently → SX11.
- **Re-pointed, still KILLED:** C2-6 and C3-7, whose source text moved.
- **All prior WR8 reproductions run on the corrected view and are KILLED:** C2-5/10, C3-3…6, C4-6…9.
- **A7b now has 66 mutants.**

**Validation (corrective 4):**
- **Harnesses: 820/820 across twelve** (catalog 102: +4, SX9–SX12).
- **Canon:** `--check` valid.
- **Builds:** **clean** Debug and **clean** Release succeed, with warnings only in the four pre-existing files. That includes your icon swap, which builds without warnings.
- **Byte-identical to freeze 3:**
  - the gameplay files (UpgradeManager, PlayerStats, GameScene, GameConfig);
  - the catalog and canon inputs (`catalog-expect.json`, the generator, the sidecar, the canon).
- **Phase:** MD1/MD2 hold. Phase at 666pt is the sole card above 665, and every card is ≤ 667.
- **The icon, `Info.plist` and workspace changes:** `git status` is identical to before this corrective, and the files are untouched.

## Independent review of freeze 4: FAIL (two test-strength blockers) → corrective 5 (Sep 29)

**The review:** freeze 4 (`29d3459e4ad18b12`) was reviewed exactly. **No production defect**; S3 stays blocked. The scope is recorded as given:

> Corrective 5 governing principle. Do not build a general Swift expression parser. For MW7 and WR8, tighten the contract: the required operation must appear as a standalone executable statement at the already-approved structural depth/path. A call merely embedded inside another expression does not satisfy the contract.
> 1. Shared standalone-call check. Add a small reusable structural helper over the existing active-code view that verifies a required call: appears in active executable code; is at the expected brace/block depth; occupies its own accepted statement line in the frozen production shape; is not preceded/followed by tokens indicating it participates in a larger expression; is not part of a ternary `?:`; remains subject to all existing wrapper/control-flow/directive/string/comment checks. It is acceptable for this regression contract to reject equivalent source reformatting and require an intentional harness update. We are proving the frozen accepted structure, not arbitrary Swift equivalence.
> 2. MW7. `apexRegisterAttack()` must be a standalone executable statement on the accepted direct-body path. The Reviewer's exact reproduction must fail: `false ?` / `apexRegisterAttack()` / `: ()`. Also retain at least: same-line ternary: `false ? apexRegisterAttack() : ()`; a multiline ternary with comments/whitespace around `?` / `:`. Preserve all prior MW7 protections …
> 3. WR8. `camera.addChild(seam)` must likewise be a standalone executable statement directly on the accepted hot-flag path. The exact Reviewer ternary reproduction must fail, plus a same-line ternary variant. Preserve all prior WR8 reachability checks …
> 4. Retained coverage. Add the two Reviewer reproductions and the equivalent same-line/multiline ternary variants as retained mutants. Before implementing, verify the Reviewer cases actually survive Freeze 4.
> Validation … verify gameplay/catalog/canon inputs remain byte-identical to Freeze 4; preserve Phase as the sole documented 666pt exception; keep Brandon's App Icon / `Info.plist` / workspace changes excluded and untouched. Cut Freeze 5 only after every new ternary reproduction is killed. Stop for independent re-review. Do not begin S3.

**Before any fix:**
- Freeze 4 was verified (28/28, digest `29d3459e4ad18b12`) and snapshotted (`a7b/snap-pre-c5`). Your unrelated changes were exactly as found.
- **Reproductions (B7-C5-1…6)** were run against freeze 4. **Five SURVIVED**, including **both of the Reviewer's exact forms**:
  - C5-1, the MW7 multi-line ternary;
  - C5-3, a multi-line ternary with comments, whitespace and a tab around `?`/`:`;
  - C5-4, the WR8 Mote-banner multi-line ternary;
  - C5-5 and C5-6, same-line ternaries on the Mote and forced-card banners.
- C5-2 (the MW7 same-line ternary) was already KILLED, incidentally, by MW6's exact-line rule. It is retained as a regression variant.

**What changed** (tools only; `a7b/corrective5.patch`, 160 lines, 5 files including the catalog README's SX row):

1. **`SwiftSource.Block.isStandaloneStatement(at:length:)`: one small shared structural helper on the executable view.** It is not an expression parser. The required call must be:
   - **alone on its own line**;
   - **not joined to the previous executable line**: that line doesn't end with `?`, `:`, `=`, a binary operator, `(`, `[`, `,`, `.` or `\`;
   - **not joined to the next**: that line starts a new statement (a letter, `_` or `}`), not `.`, `:`, `?`, a trailing-closure `{` or an operator.

   Comments and tabs between the tokens change nothing, because comments are already blanks. As the ruling allows, an equivalent but reformatted statement fails on purpose.
2. **MW7:** each registration must also be standalone. All prior protections are unchanged: executable view (strings, comments, inactive directives), depth 1, the accepted control-flow skeleton, and Apex before Erasure.
3. **WR8:** each banner's `camera.addChild(seam)` must also be standalone. All prior reachability checks are unchanged: active hot branch, executable text/attach directly in that branch, exit-free path, `#if DEBUG` placement.
4. **Fixture SX13:**
   - **accepted:** a standalone call, the call with a trailing comment, after an assignment line, after an `if` block;
   - **rejected:** a same-line ternary; the Reviewer's multi-line ternary; a previous line ending in `?` (including after a comment); a next line beginning with `:` (including after a tab); chaining with `.`; a trailing closure; an assignment on the line or across lines; an argument of another call; an operand of an operator.

**Retained coverage:**
- **B7-C5-1…6:** all KILLED (MW7, and WR8 for the banners).
- **Mutants of the helper itself**, each KILLED by SX13:
  - C5-7: the helper ignores what shares the line;
  - C5-8: it ignores a joining previous line;
  - C5-9: it ignores a joining next line.
- **A7b now has 75 mutants, all KILLED.** Every prior A7b mutant is retained.

**Validation (corrective 5):**

- **Canon:** `--check` valid.
- **Builds:** **clean** Debug and **clean** Release succeed, with warnings only in the four pre-existing files.

## Independent review of freeze 5: FAIL (one test-strength class) → corrective 6 (Sep 29)

**The review:** freeze 5 (`1d2bde923d7109f0`) was reviewed exactly. **No production defect**; S3 stays blocked. The scope is recorded as given:

> Corrective 6 architecture. Stop extending `isStandaloneStatement` to infer arbitrary Swift expression semantics. The accepted source shape may be pinned. Add an exact active-executable token-prefix tripwire for MW7 and WR8. Use the existing SwiftSource lexical / conditional-compilation machinery to produce active source, then tokenize the relevant region while: ignoring whitespace; ignoring comments; excluding inactive conditional-compilation regions; preserving all executable token lexemes that can affect semantics, including identifiers, keywords, operators, punctuation and literals. Do not normalize away semantic token differences. The check is intentionally structural and may reject semantically equivalent source refactors until explicitly re-approved.
> MW7: For each protected gun-hit registration path, pin the exact accepted executable token sequence from the relevant function-body entry / stable anchor through and including the required registration call. A mutation introducing any executable structure before or around the call must change the sequence and fail. The Reviewer's exact reproduction must fail: `assert(true, String(describing:` / `try` / `apexRegisterAttack()` / `as Void` / `))`. Preserve the independent registration-count and ordering assertions. Retain all prior MW7 mutants.
> WR8: Pin the accepted executable token sequence from the intended hot-flag branch entry / stable anchor through and including `camera.addChild(seam)`. The Reviewer's lazy `assert` argument wrapper around that call must fail. Preserve the existing checks proving: correct hot flag; active DEBUG region; banner text; no early exit; correct attachment target. Retain all prior WR8 mutants.
> Fingerprint form: Prefer a SHA-256 or exact token-array comparison generated from the accepted baseline. If using a hash, document the accepted token range and expected digest beside the check so a future intentional source change requires an explicit test-contract update. Do not hash the entire GameScene or entire function if a stable prefix through the required call is sufficient. Keep the tripwire narrowly scoped to the behavior being protected.
> Before fixing: Reproduce both Reviewer's `assert(... String(describing: try ... as Void))` cases against Freeze 5 and confirm they survive. Add them permanently as retained mutants. Add at least one formatting/comment variant demonstrating that harmless whitespace/comment changes do not alter the token tripwire. … Cut Freeze 6 only after both new reproductions are killed. Stop for independent re-review. Do not begin S3.

**Before any fix:**
- Freeze 5 was verified (28/28, digest `1d2bde923d7109f0`) and snapshotted (`a7b/snap-pre-c6`). Your unrelated changes were exactly as found.
- **Reproductions (B7-C6-1…3)** were run against freeze 5. **All three SURVIVED:**
  - C6-1: the MW7 `assert(… try apexRegisterAttack() as Void)` wrapper;
  - C6-2: the same wrapper around the Mote banner's `camera.addChild(seam)`;
  - C6-3: the same wrapper around the forced-card banner's.

**What changed** (tools only; `a7b/corrective6.patch`, 349 lines, 5 files including the catalog README row):

1. **`SwiftSource` gains an exact active-token view. Nothing new is inferred.**
   - The lexer now records each **outermost string literal's span**, delimiters included; interpolations and any nested literal stay inside it.
   - `Block.active` is the code view (comments blanked, strings intact) with **inactive `#if` regions and directive lines blanked**. It uses the same line decisions as the executable view.
   - `Block.tokens(from:to:)` returns the exact token sequence. Whitespace and comments are ignored and inactive regions excluded. **Every other lexeme is kept verbatim:** identifiers, keywords, numbers (`1_000`, `0x1F`, `2.5`), operator runs (`??`, `==`), punctuation, and **each string literal whole**, including its internal whitespace and interpolations. Nothing semantic is normalized away.
   - `SwiftSource.digest` is SHA-256 over the tokens joined by U+001F, so token boundaries are part of the fingerprint.
2. **MW8 (redsmile), the MW7 tripwire.** Each protected registration path is pinned as token count + SHA-256.
   - **Range:** from the hit function's body `{` through and including the registration call, and nothing after it. The whole pre-call body decides whether the call is reached, and a stable prefix through the call is what the ruling allows; it is not the whole function or `GameScene`.

     | Path | Tokens | SHA-256 |
     |---|---|---|
     | `handleProjectileHit` → `apexRegisterAttack()` | 1010 | `c7a67a795290a67b00e7e65ea3109f7fce871ee2d3f9638e6470b0b69de1f2c9` |
     | `handleProjectileHit` → `erasureRegisterHit()` | 1013 | `5b43f49924328e3c1ff74a9c7ce56e6f0bd9d0b2641cdb47bcb8c0f46c236bf6` |
     | `handleProjectileHitBoss` → `erasureRegisterHit()` | 378 | `65df1de421ed212d7a01ce41dc0753effee98871f16d67155912da4224ae4d97` |

   - These values are documented beside the check. Any executable change before or around a registration fails, so an intentional source change must update them here as a reviewed contract change. **S3 will be the first such update**, because its planned G1.1 adds The Hunter's registration to `handleProjectileHitBoss`.
   - MW6 (registration count, exact lines) and MW7 (executable, standalone, depth 1, the control-flow skeleton, Apex before Erasure) are **kept unchanged**.
3. **The WR8 tripwire (catalog).** Each banner branch is pinned as an **exact token array**, from the hot-flag `if` through and including `camera.addChild(seam)`: 83 tokens for the forced card, 82 for Mote. They're readable in `bannerTokens(forced:)`.
   - Every prior WR8 check is kept: the correct hot flag, the active DEBUG region, the banner text, an exit-free path, a standalone attach to the correct target.
4. **Fixtures:**
   - **SX14:** the tokenizer.
     - A mixed line of strings, comments, numbers, operators and a raw string tokenizes exactly as expected.
     - Whitespace and newlines don't matter, but whitespace inside a string literal does.
     - Interpolated and multi-line literals are whole tokens, and inactive regions are excluded.
     - `==` and `=` differ, and `try f() as Void` stays verbatim.
     - The digest separates `["ab","c"]` from `["a","bc"]`.
   - **SX15, the formatting demonstration on the real `GameScene`:**
     - Harmless edits leave **both tripwires unchanged**: comments, blank lines, tab indentation, `seam.zPosition   =   300` spacing, and a block comment before the attach.
     - The lazy `assert(… try … as Void)` wrapper **changes both**.
     - It's an executed check, not a mutant, because the retained suite only counts kills and a harmless variant must pass.

**Retained coverage:**
- **B7-C6-1…3, the Reviewer's reproductions:** all KILLED (MW8; WR8).
- **Machinery mutants**, all KILLED:
  - C6-4: the tokenizer splits string literals → SX14, WR8.
  - C6-5: the tokenizer reads inactive regions → SX14.
  - C6-6: the digest drops token boundaries → SX14.
- **Every prior A7b mutant is retained. A7b now has 81, all KILLED.**
- **A harness-ordering note:** SX15 reads the scene path directly. My first draft called the harness's `raw(_:)` helper, which reads the top-level `root` declared later in `main.swift`, so it crashed at run time (top-level globals initialize in source order). That was caught before the freeze.

## Independent review of freeze 8: ✅ PASS WITH A NON-BLOCKING FINDING → S4 ACCEPTED (Sep 30)

**Brandon / Command Center (Sep 30), recorded as given:**

> S4 accepted. Begin S5.
> Freeze 8 `26895344b3a4bd01` is the accepted S4 baseline.
> Record the independent Reviewer's WR10 finding as a non-blocking banked test-tooling item:
>
> * initializer/reset config assertions can be satisfied by expected assignments inside inactive `#if false` while active code uses a literal;
> * current production initialization/reset are correct;
> * retain both Reviewer reproductions for future tooling cleanup;
> * do not address this in S5 unless S5 directly touches the affected machinery.
>
> S5 scope remains only the snowman / Overload stun coexistence seam.
> Implement the already-ruled behavior:
>
> * snowman state and Overload stun are independent control holds;
> * ending snowman must not clear an active Overload stun;
> * becoming a snowman must not overwrite or repurpose the Overload stun timer;
> * effective stunned state is true while either hold is active;
> * an Overload stun applied during snowman may continue for its remaining duration after the snowman ends;
> * existing Overload durations, immunity timing, melt behavior, and unrelated control mechanics remain unchanged except where required by that coexistence rule.
>
> Build the bounded tests/mutants, freeze, and route for independent review.
> Do not start S6.
> After S5 passes, stop for the approved S3-S5 Brandon device gate.

**Freeze 8 (`26895344b3a4bd01`) is the accepted S4 baseline.** It verified 30/30 before S5 began, the tree state matched it, and its manifest and snapshot are kept. The pre-S5 snapshot is `a7b/snap-pre-s5/`.

**BT-3 (banked test-tooling item; non-blocking): WR10's initializer/reset assertions read the code view, not the executable view.**
- **The gap:** WR10 checks `terraSlow`'s initializer and reset as text in the code view, which still contains inactive `#if false` regions. So an expected assignment parked in `#if false`, with a literal active in `#else`, satisfies it. GW can't tell either, because the literal equals the constant today.
- **Production is correct:** `var terraSlow: CGFloat = GameConfig.Growth.enemySlow` and the reset `terraSlow = GameConfig.Growth.enemySlow` are both active code.
- **The two reproductions are retained:** reconstructed from the finding as `a7b/bt3-repro.sh`, and re-run on throwaway copies on Sep 30. Both SURVIVE (`a7b/bt3-repro.out`), confirming the finding.
  1. **Initializer:** replace `    var terraSlow: CGFloat = GameConfig.Growth.enemySlow` with `#if false` / that line / `#else` / `    var terraSlow: CGFloat = 0.30` / `#endif`.
  2. **Reset:** replace `        terraSlow = GameConfig.Growth.enemySlow` the same way, with `terraSlow = 0.30` active.
  - These are my reconstruction from the finding's description. If the Reviewer's exact text differs, it replaces these.
- **Future cleanup:** move those two assertions onto the executable view (as WR9–WR11's token checks already are), then turn both reproductions into retained mutants.
- **Not addressed in S5:** S5 doesn't touch WR10 or `PlayerStats`. S5's own EnemyNode pins (WR11) are on the executable view from the start, so this class doesn't apply to them.

## Build — S5: G1.7 the snowman and the Overload stun are independent holds (Sep 30)

**Scope, exactly the coexistence seam.**
- No Overload duration, immunity timing or melt behaviour changes: O1–O7, S1–S2 and SH3's melt all pass unchanged.
- No card copy changes; the sidecar prose is updated to current behaviour.
- S6 is not started.

### Production (1 new file + EnemyNode; both pinned)

| Change | Where |
|---|---|
| **New pure `StunHold`:** the TIMED stun on game time. `stun(_:)` keeps the longer duration (the existing `max` rule); `tick(_:)` is the existing plain subtraction; `isStunned(snowman:)` is true while the timed stun runs OR the snowman form holds. There is deliberately no way to clear it early. | `Sparkforge/Systems/StunHold.swift` (new; the project's synchronized groups pick it up) |
| `stunTimer` → `stunHold`. `isStunned` = `stunHold.isStunned(snowman: snowman.isSnowman)`. `applyStun` and `applyOverloadStun` call `stunHold.stun(duration)`; the status tick calls `stunHold.tick(deltaTime)`. **`becomeSnowman` no longer writes the timed stun** (its now-unused `applied` binding is dropped). **`endSnowman` no longer zeroes it.** | `Sparkforge/Nodes/EnemyNode.swift` (+13 / −14) |

**Effect.**
- **Sequence A:** an Overload stun that lands on a snowman now runs its full remaining time after the form ends. The dazed stars and the body's stun end together, and the 3s immunity starts then, exactly as before.
- **Sequence B:** a same-hit Overload on a melting T3 elite now holds it 0.25s / 0.5s after the melt. Before, the melt zeroed it: the proc was wasted, but its immunity still started.
- **Becoming a snowman over an Overload stun** leaves that stun's timer untouched, so Overload ends and starts its immunity on time while the form holds the body.

**One consequence to flag (R2, same timer).** A panda's prune stun (0.3s) and Erasure's Phase Lock (1s) share the timed stun with Overload through `applyStun`.
- So they also stop being cut short when a snowman they overlap ends. They run their remaining time: the same "can't cut short another control" rule, and the recon's approved fix.
- Keeping the old cut for those two only would need a second timer to preserve an R2 violation, so I didn't build that.
- **If the Command Center wants it anyway, that's a ruling for later**, not S5 scope. Nothing else about either control changes.

**Canon.**
- Sidecar prose, current behaviour: `shock_4` and `v16_whiteout` sensitivities. Both described the old cut.
- Regenerated; `--check docs/v2.1-ability-canon.md` passes: valid, 80 cards, 21 synergy tiers.

### New and changed checks (+6: 842 → 848)

| Harness | Checks | What they prove |
|---|---|---|
| shock | **SH1–SH5** (new; the run.sh also compiles `SnowmanState.swift` and `StunHold.swift`) | Executed on the REAL `StunHold`, `SnowmanState` and `OverloadStunState`, composed in EnemyNode's order, in binary-exact 1/64s frames. **SH1:** either hold stuns; the timed stun keeps the longer; it ends on game time. **SH2 (Sequence A):** a 1s Overload at 2.5s on a 3s snowman holds the body to 3.5s, and immunity starts at 3.5s. **SH3 (Sequence B):** a same-hit Overload on a melting T3 elite holds 0.5s after the melt; the melt is unchanged (+20% max HP, form consumed). **SH4:** becoming a snowman leaves the 1s timer at 1s (not 3s); Overload and its immunity end/start on time; the form alone holds the body to 3s. **SH5:** unchanged without a timed stun, so the form holds exactly its duration and a melted elite is free at once. |
| catalog | **WR11** (new) | On EnemyNode's EXECUTABLE view: no `stunTimer`; `isStunned` is exactly either hold; `stunHold` at exactly 5 sites (declaration, `isStunned`, `applyStun`, `applyOverloadStun`, the status tick), each stun call and the tick a standalone depth-1 statement. Overload's hold is the exact tokens right after its immunity guard. `becomeSnowman` and `endSnowman` contain no `stunHold` (and no `stunTimer`). |
| void | WR23 (re-pointed) | The Void trap never touches the timed stun: it now looks for `stunHold`, the renamed timer (it would otherwise pass vacuously). |

- **Retained mutant re-pointed:** `B7-S0a-4` (the S0a proof that the suite mutates `Sparkforge/Nodes`) matched `stunTimer = max(stunTimer, duration)`; it now matches `stunHold.stun(duration)`, with the same mutation and the same kill (void WR22).
- **Why the split:** SH executes the rule, WR11 pins that EnemyNode actually wires it that way. `Body` in SH is a composition model of that wiring, and WR11 is what keeps the model honest. That's why every EnemyNode-side mutant below is a WR11 kill.

### Retained mutants: B7-S5-1…11 (suite 315 → 326)

All 11 were **KILLED** by assertions; none were INVALID or TIMEOUT. The re-pointed `B7-S0a-4` is still KILLED (void WR22). `python3 tools/catalog-harness/mutations.py B7-S5` runs them.

| Id | Mutant | Killed by |
|---|---|---|
| S5-1 | ending a snowman clears the timed stun again (the pre-S5 bug) | catalog WR11 |
| S5-2 | becoming a snowman writes its duration into the timed stun again (the pre-S5 code) | WR11 |
| S5-3 | `EnemyNode.isStunned` ignores the snowman hold | WR11 |
| S5-4 | a snowman pauses the timed stun (`if !isSnowman { stunHold.tick… }`) | WR11 |
| S5-5 | Overload shows its stars but never holds the body | WR11 |
| S5-6 | the status tick parked in `#if false` | WR11 |
| S5-7 | a DEBUG-only reset in `endSnowman` (active in the proved configuration) | WR11 |
| S5-8 | the Void trap writes the timed stun (WR23 re-pointed) | void WR23 |
| S5-9 | `StunHold`: the snowman hold no longer stuns | shock SH1, SH4, SH5 |
| S5-10 | `StunHold`: a new timed stun overwrites a longer one | SH1 |
| S5-11 | `StunHold`: the timed stun runs at half speed | SH1, SH2, SH3, SH4 |

S5-1 and S5-2 together are the pre-S5 EnemyNode.
