## Build — S6: G1.8 independent vulnerability channels (CL-107 + CL-116) · G2-5 the Apex T4 face (Sep 30)

**No new player-facing question surfaced.** Every behaviour below is CL-107, CL-116 or one of CL-116's recorded build readings:
- Marked's "only if the slot is 1.0" gate becomes "only if not already Marked";
- Marked lasts until death;
- each source keeps its own timing;
- Frostbite is set on open and cleared on close;
- the boss-class halved values keep their order;
- the Spurhound/Ramplate ×1.5 punish window stays outside the channels.

No decision row is needed.

### Production (2 new/changed Systems files, 8 node files, the scene, the Apex face)

| Change | Where |
|---|---|
| **New pure `VulnerabilityChannels`:** four stored channels (Frostbite, Marked, Called, Fracture; 1.0 = none), `set(_:_:)` / `clear(_:)` touching only the named channel, `isActive(_:)`, and the ONE resolution `multiplier = max(1.0, all four)`. **New `VulnerabilityCarrier` protocol:** a body's channel store, plus the one shared accessor `vulnerabilityMultiplier { vulnerability.multiplier }`, so no body re-implements the resolution. | `Sparkforge/Systems/VulnerabilityChannels.swift` (new) |
| `EnemyNode` adopts `VulnerabilityCarrier`; `var vulnerabilityMultiplier = 1.0` → `var vulnerability = VulnerabilityChannels()`. `applyFracture` sets `.fracture`; its expiry clears `.fracture`; Frostbite's window sets `.frostbite` on open and clears `.frostbite` on close. | `Nodes/EnemyNode.swift` |
| `ArenaBossNode` inherits `VulnerabilityCarrier` (the `vulnerabilityMultiplier { get set }` requirement is gone). Each of the six conformers swaps its stored multiplier for `var vulnerability = VulnerabilityChannels()`: BossNode, QuenchWarden, DynamoChoir, FacetedLie, MonumentBoss, Marchwarden. | `Nodes/ArenaBossNode.swift` + the six boss files (1 line each) |
| Called: `set(.called, …)` on the prey (enemy and boss), re-written each attached frame as before; `clearCalled` clears `.called` only. Marked: `set(.marked, …)` behind `!isActive(.marked)` (enemy after its 10s, the arena boss at once, as before); never cleared. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **G2-5:** the Apex T4 face `Marked: foes alive 10s take +35% damage` → **`Marked: bosses at once, foes after 10s: +35%`**. The runtime is unchanged: the boss is Marked at once, foes after 10s. It fits: 3/3 face lines, the card-detail modal ≤ 667pt, and Phase at 666pt still the only card above 665 (MD0–MD2). | `Systems/UpgradeManager.swift` |

**All seven `takeDamage` consumers are byte-identical:** EnemyNode and the six bosses. Each reads `vulnerabilityMultiplier`, now the shared resolved accessor, once in its scaling statement. **The per-source values are unchanged** (GameConfig untouched): Frostbite ×2.0, Marked ×1.35, Called ×1.35, Fracture ×1.4; boss-class ×1.5 / ×1.175 / ×1.175 / ×1.2.

**What players get (all CL-107/116, all ruled):**
- **Stronger marks survive:** Called no longer downgrades a Frostbitten prey to +35% while lassoed; it stays at +100%.
- **Ending Called no longer wipes a Frostbite window.**
- **Fracture and Frostbite** no longer erase each other at expiry.
- **Marked no longer waits:** it lands at 10s even on a body already Called or Fractured, where before it waited for an empty slot.

**Canon.**
- The Apex T4 face is in the catalog, so the canon's copy line updates.
- Sidecar prose, current behaviour: `cap_bleed_apex` (hit class: "foes after 10s alive, the arena boss at once"; sensitivities), `cap_chill_polarvortex`, `cap_shock_skybeam` and `cap_void_erasure` (sensitivities; Erasure's rulings gain CL-107 and CL-116).
- Regenerated; `--check docs/v2.1-ability-canon.md` passes: valid, 80 cards, 21 synergy tiers.
- Before regenerating, `--check` failed on those entries plus the fingerprint.

### New and changed checks (+11: 848 → 859)

| Harness | Checks | What they prove |
|---|---|---|
| damage-pipeline | **VC0–VC9** (new; the run.sh compiles `VulnerabilityChannels.swift`) | Executed on the REAL `VulnerabilityChannels` / `VulnerabilityCarrier`, with the REAL per-source values: Fracture and BossClass extracted; Frostbite, Called and Marked read from the app's `GameConfig.swift`, since their Stubs mirrors lack them. **VC0:** values unchanged, boss-class halved. **VC1:** a fresh store carries nothing; each source activates its own channel alone. **VC2:** all four active gives the strongest (×2.0), not the product (×5.103) or the sum (×3.1). **VC3:** the weaker channels stay stored underneath. **VC4:** clearing reveals the next strongest, ×2.0 → ×1.4 → ×1.35 → ×1.35 → none. **VC5:** no cross-clearing, for every source. **VC6:** all 24 acquisition orders and a per-frame Called re-write give the same store. **VC7:** lifetimes on EnemyNode's own timers, the recon's real losses reversed (Called never downgrades Frostbite; letting go keeps it; Frostbite closing reveals Fracture; Fracture's expiry ends at none). **VC8:** Marked lands on a Called + Fractured body and outlives both. **VC9:** the boss-class order holds, and a boss body resolves exactly as an enemy body (one accessor). |
| catalog | **WR12** (new) | On the EXECUTABLE view of EVERY app source file. **One resolution:** `vulnerability.multiplier` is read only by the shared accessor, no raw channel is read anywhere else, and only that accessor declares `vulnerabilityMultiplier`. **One model:** EnemyNode and ArenaBossNode adopt the carrier, and exactly the seven bodies declare a fresh store, with no other `vulnerability =` assignment anywhere. **Applied once:** each body's `takeDamage` scales in its accepted statement, and the reads of the resolved value are exactly those (EnemyNode's third is `isVulnerable`). **Ten writes:** the channel-write census is exactly the ten accepted statements (4 in EnemyNode, 6 in the scene, Marked behind its own gate). |
| catalog | CP1 (updated) | Pins the approved G2-5 face (the one A7a-accepted string that S6 changes, as approved). |

**"Reset between runs/enemies," concretely:**
- Every EnemyNode and every boss spawns with a fresh store (VC1; WR12 pins the fresh initializer on all seven bodies and forbids any other reassignment).
- No store outlives its body. The scene's weak `calledEnemy`/`calledBoss` references are cleared with the lasso, as before.

## Independent review of freeze 10: FAIL (proof strength only; no production defect) → corrective 7 (Sep 30)

**Routed by Mote for Brandon (Sep 30), recorded in substance:**
- **Verdict:** the independent Reviewer ("Sparkforge S3 Unit 7b | Reviewer", Codex thread `01a0f000-172d-7b13-ac6f-240765aaf49f`, final review turn `01a0f449-7f68-7f41-ad06-108a2fe7eb41`) returned **FAIL, test/proof strength only; no production defect found**.
- **Freeze 10 is immutable:** 41 files, digest `07a8d548840bf565956f0a79dbd2ee16d9f791bc94ec5356cec6377fe6276eb7`. Its manifest, snapshot and all review evidence are preserved.
- **The two WR12 proof gaps:**
  1. The writer census proves a channel write is present, not that it's reached or cleared on the right tick: an early return before Fracture's write, or a negated expiry test, both survive.

### Production (3 changed files + 1 new; all pinned)

| Change | Where |
|---|---|
| **New pure `DirectHitDamage`:** `resolve(prefix, amplifiers, rounding:)` = CL-70's `VoidRounding.damage(prefix × product, unit:)`, so ONE unbiased rounding, minimum 1, and with no amplifier exactly the prefix. **`DirectHitAmplifiers`** holds the ruled applicability as two builders. `onEnemy` gives Permafrost on any slow, the arena-wide one included (Q-C5); Brittle Cold on a slowed, frozen or stunned foe; Open Wounds on a bleeding foe. `onBoss` gives Open Wounds only. **`DirectHitRounding`** is the block's own threshold, a fresh uniform draw unless injected. | `Sparkforge/Systems/DirectHitDamage.swift` (new) |
| **All four chains:** the three truncating amplifier `if` blocks (one on the boss chains) are gone. Forge offense now ends the integer prefix, and ONE `damage = DirectHitDamage.resolve(damage, .onEnemy(…) / .onBoss(…), rounding: …)` follows it. The integer prefix (base, crit, Lucky Break, executes), the Braceguard suffix, every rider and the consumption are byte-identical in tokens. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` (`handleProjectileHit`, `handleProjectileHitBoss`, `redSmileHit`, `redSmileHitBoss`) |
| **The threshold source:** a shot carries `let hitRounding = DirectHitRounding()`, drawn at launch beside, and independent of, its `a6Rounding`. A sweep draws `DirectHitRounding()` per target. | `Nodes/ProjectileNode.swift` (+4 lines) |
| **G2-1:** Permafrost's face is `Hits deal +25% to slowed enemies`; its detail is `Your projectiles and Red Smile sweeps deal 25% more damage to slowed enemies, whatever slowed them. Damage between whole numbers rounds up by chance.` Both fit the card and modal limits (catalog CP1/CP4, MD0–MD2; chill E6). | `Systems/UpgradeManager.swift` |

## Independent review of freeze 12: FAIL (proof strength only) → corrective 8 (Sep 30)

**Brandon's instruction (Sep 30), recorded as given:** "Correct S7's two proof gaps using /private/tmp/sparkforge-s7-independent-review/reproductions.json and escaped-regressions.json. Strengthen executed real-projectile threshold/distribution tests; retain both reproductions and all 363 existing mutants. Preserve production, config, canon and sidecar bytes against Freeze12. Run full validation, create a new freeze, and stop for the same independent Reviewer. No S8, commits or pushes."

**The two gaps** (from the Reviewer's `reproductions.json` / `escaped-regressions.json`, read and not modified). Both compiled and passed all 883 checks plus the canon.

| Id | Mutant (the Reviewer's exact strings) | Why it escaped |
|---|---|---|
| `rng-two-point` | `DirectHitDamage.swift`: `init(unit: CGFloat = CGFloat.random(in: 0..<1))` → `init(unit: CGFloat = Bool.random() ? 0.0 : 0.999999)` | DH7 checked only the mean, the extremes and the correlation, and a two-point draw satisfies all three. |
| `projectile-discard-draw` | `ProjectileNode.swift`: `let hitRounding = DirectHitRounding()` → `let hitRounding = DirectHitRounding().unit >= 0 ? DirectHitRounding(unit: 0.5) : DirectHitRounding()` | WR13 matched the declaration as a substring, and nothing executed a real projectile's threshold. |

**Both survive unchanged freeze 12.**
- **Entry checks:** freeze 12 verified 46/46 against the tree and its snapshot, with the tree and user-owned state matching it. The pre-corrective snapshot is `a7b/snap-pre-c8/`.
- **Re-run:** the two reproductions in disposable copies made from that snapshot by its own `mutations.py`, over all 13 harnesses plus `canon-file` (`a7b/c8/repro-survival.py` → `a7b/c8/survival-before.json`). Both **SURVIVED**.

### The correction (test-only)

- **vulnerability harness, RP1–RP4 (new):**
  - **What it compiles:** the REAL `ProjectileNode`, plus `KillSource`, `DirectHitDamage` and the extracted `GameConfig.Projectile`, beside the real EnemyNode and bosses it already compiled.
  - **What it runs:** 20,000 real projectiles built through their real initializer, the moment a shot is launched.
  - **RP1:** each shot's `hitRounding` is FRESH and in [0, 1) (20,000 of 20,000 distinct).
  - **RP2:** it is UNIFORM. The Kolmogorov–Smirnov distance to U(0, 1) is under 0.02 (0.0102 measured), and the CDF at every decile is within 0.02.
  - **RP3:** it is INDEPENDENT of the same shot's A6 threshold: |r| < 0.04 (0.0048 measured), and every cell of the joint 4×4 grid is within 6σ of 1250.
  - **RP4:** rounding a 1-damage Permafrost hit through the real block on each real shot's threshold gives 1 or 2, averaging ×1.25 (1.2442 measured).
  - **Statistics:** this samples the production RNG, unseeded by design. ~~Every bound sits ≥ 5σ out, so the false-failure odds are well under one in a million.~~ A constant, two-point or copied draw misses each bound by a wide margin.
    - **Corrected Oct 1, per the independent re-review of freeze 13** (turn `01a0f5ba-b688-7ae3-adfa-138f97ae0bbc`): the struck sentence understated the risk.
      - DH7's retained `|r| < 0.03` over 20,000 samples is about **4.24σ**: an approximate **correlation-only** false-failure probability of **1 in 45,000 per invocation**.
      - DH7's mean bound is about **4.90σ**.
      - This is not an overall suite failure-rate estimate, and no flake occurred in the reported validation.
- **damage-pipeline DH7 (strengthened; strictly additive):**
  - every original conjunct is kept verbatim: in range, mean ≈ ½, min < 0.01, max > 0.99, |r| < 0.03;
  - added: Kolmogorov–Smirnov < 0.02, every decile within 0.02, and a 1-damage Permafrost hit rounded on 20,000 default draws averaging ×1.25.
- **catalog WR13 (tightened):** the projectile's two threshold declarations must each be the WHOLE executable line, `let hitRounding = DirectHitRounding()` and `let a6Rounding = A6Rounding()`, with `hitRounding` on exactly one line. Nothing else in WR13 changed, including every token and digest pin.

**Retained mutants: the Reviewer's two, verbatim (suite 363 → 365).** Both are **KILLED by assertions**; none are INVALID or TIMEOUT.

| Id | The Reviewer's mutant | Before (freeze 12) | Now: killed by |
|---|---|---|---|
| **B7-S7R-1** | `rng-two-point` | SURVIVED all 13 + canon | damage-pipeline **DH7**; vulnerability **RP1, RP2, RP3, RP4** |
| **B7-S7R-2** | `projectile-discard-draw` | SURVIVED all 13 + canon | vulnerability **RP1, RP2, RP3, RP4**; catalog **WR13** |

All 363 freeze-12 mutants are retained and still KILLED. B7-S7-1 and B7-S7-9 now also fall to the strengthened DH7.

**The Reviewer's verdict** (the same independent Reviewer, Codex `01a0f000-172d-7b13-ac6f-240765aaf49f`, terminal report of `2026-10-01T04:58:46.457Z`, turn `01a0f5ba-b688-7ae3-adfa-138f97ae0bbc`, recorded in substance): **PASS WITH NON-BLOCKING FINDINGS** on freeze 13, `702f436a243df2531d2519b22fcbbcf9d75376283a1ff27d048180bad9c5b088` (46 files). Corrective 8 closes both blocking proof gaps, and no production defect was found.
- **Fresh isolated validation:**
  - **887/887** checks across 13 harnesses;
  - **365/365** mutants assertion-killed, all 363 prior definitions retained, with zero survivors, invalids or timeouts;
  - canon PASS;
  - clean Debug and Release PASS, with the same 29 warnings and no new DEBUG leakage.
- **Both escaped regressions now assertion-fail:**
  - the two-point default draw fails DH7 and RP1–RP4;
  - the projectile discarding its draw fails RP1–RP4 and WR13;
  - a separate copied-A6-threshold probe failed RP3's independence check.

  - freeze 13 verified 46/46 before and after the review; freezes 10–12 are intact;
  - production, config, canon and sidecar match freeze 12; the banked findings are untouched;

**The non-blocking finding: the statistical claim was overstated.**
- **What it was:** corrective 8's documentation said every bound sat ≥ 5σ out, with false-failure odds well under one in a million. That was overstated.
- **The Reviewer's figures for DH7's retained bound:** `|r| < 0.03` over 20,000 samples is about **4.24σ**, an approximate **correlation-only false-failure probability of 1 in 45,000 per invocation**. Its mean bound is about **4.90σ**. This is not an overall suite failure-rate estimate, and no flake occurred in the reported validation.
- **Where it's corrected:** in place in corrective 8's "Statistics" bullet above, struck through and annotated rather than rewritten, so the history is preserved.
- **Non-blocking follow-up, not done in this closeout:** the frozen test comment at `tools/damage-pipeline-harness/main.swift:561` ("Bounds sit ≥ 5σ out over 20,000 production draws") needs the same qualification. ### Production: CL-114 (a)–(d), as ruled; no new player-facing decision

| Change | Where |
|---|---|
| **94b, the vulnerability fold (CL-114b):** the block takes the target's resolved vulnerability (CL-107/116's strongest channel) into the SAME one rounding. It returns a `DirectHit`: **`dealt`** = ⌈⌊prefix × amplifiers × Overcharge × vulnerability⌉⌋ (one unbiased rounding, minimum 1), and **`basis`** = the same block without the vulnerability, on the same threshold. `DirectHit.shield(by:)` is Braceguard's halving on both values, exactly today's `max(1, Int(x × 0.5))`. S7's `resolve(_:_:rounding:)` stays as the same block with both factors at 1. | `Systems/DirectHitDamage.swift` |
| **94c, the Overcharge split (CL-114a):** `OverchargeSplit`. The chain floors the Overcharge-free part (`base` = ⌊B·s⌋, minimum 1), and Overcharge rides the block as `factor`, which brings that base to **max(today's floor, ⌊B·s⌋ + O·s)** EXACTLY (an ulp-safe `nextUp` guard), so no shot's base ever pays below today's floor. `PlayerStats.overchargeFreeDamageMultiplier` is today's sum without Overcharge (same terms, same order), and `overchargeParts(scale:)` gives today's whole multiplier, the free part and Overcharge's share. | `Systems/DirectHitDamage.swift`, `Systems/PlayerStats.swift` |
| **Where the split applies (CL-114a):** every `fireProjectile` shot and the icicle (`fireIcicle`) store the Overcharge-free multiplier and the split's factor (`ProjectileNode.overchargeFactor`); both Red Smile sweeps start from the split's base. **Not** acorns, seed fragments, Glass Blood or the Void secondaries (they keep today's whole multiplier and factor 1), nor `shotFractionDamage`'s other users (Electro Pulse, Static Crown, Sentry). `shotBaseDamage` is byte-identical, so the Void FX6/XS pins stand. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift`, `Nodes/ProjectileNode.swift` |
| **The direct entry (CL-114c):** `EnemyNode.takeDirectHit(_:)`, and the bosses' `takeDirectHit(_:ignoresChallengeDEF:)` (defaulting `false`), land `dealt` as is, with no second in-node vulnerability. Every other source keeps `takeDamage` and its in-node rounding. The boss protocol's one body takes `resolved: Int?`, and the DEF dial still follows and still reads the pre-vulnerability request (`basis`) for its execute bypass, exactly as `raw` did. **The Spurhound / Ramplate punish window** moves to the direct entry unchanged (both entries route through one `punished(_:)` with today's arithmetic). | `Nodes/EnemyNode.swift`, `ArenaBossNode.swift`, the six bosses (one line each), `SpurhoundNode.swift`, `RamplateNode.swift` |
| **The four chains:** Forge, then `var/let hit = DirectHitDamage.resolve(…, overcharge: <factor>, vulnerability: <target>.vulnerabilityMultiplier, rounding: …)`; the enemy chains then Braceguard `hit.shield(by:)`, then `damage = hit.basis` (**CL-114d**: Overkill, Chain Lightning and the sweep's return keep the pre-vulnerability basis); then `takeDirectHit(hit…)`. The integer prefix, every rider, the meters and the ruled Shatter exits are unchanged. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |

**Size:** GameScene +39/−16, DirectHitDamage +76/−7, EnemyNode +21/−3, ArenaBossNode +20/−1, PlayerStats +19/−0, Spurhound and Ramplate +11/−2 each, ProjectileNode +6/−0, the six bosses +2/−2 or +3/−3 each. **GameConfig is byte-identical.**

**The accepted CL-114 side effects** (ruled; for the playtest):
- **Vulnerability on low hits:** a 1-damage gun or sweep hit on a Frostbitten mini-boss now averages 1.5 (it was 2, from in-node round-half-up); on a Fractured foe, 1.4 (it was 1). Marked and Called now move 1-damage hits on average too.
- **Overcharge:** it now lifts 1-damage shots (+5…+50% by chance), and an Iron Skin / Everglow integer crossing never drops below its old floor.
- **The punish windows:** Spurhound and Ramplate now apply their ×1.5 after the folded vulnerability rather than before it (the same arithmetic, on the direct entry).
- **No unruled player-facing question surfaced in S8.**

**Canon.** Sidecar prose made true by S8: `cap_bleed_apex` (Marked), `cap_shock_skybeam` (Called), `v13_overcharge`, `void_1`, and the crosscutting "gun damage is a whole number" rule. Regenerated; `--check` valid (80 cards, 21 synergy tiers).

| Harness | Checks | What they prove |
|---|---|---|
| damage-pipeline | **DH9–DH12** (new) | **DH9:** the vulnerability folds into the one rounding (dealt = ⌊x⌋/⌈x⌉, basis ≤ dealt, equal at ×1), with the CL-114 side effects measured (1.5 / 1.4). **DH10:** the split over 56,529 integer-crossing cases at the real damage scales: base, factor ≥ 1, EXACTLY max(today, ⌊B·s⌋ + O·s), and never below today even at the highest unit; no Overcharge = no change; a 1-damage shot averages 1 + O. **DH11:** the real PlayerStats: the whole multiplier is bit-identical to today's, the parts sum to it, and `shotFractionDamage` is unchanged. **DH12:** Braceguard's halving on both values. |
| vulnerability | **RN4–RN5** (new) | On the REAL EnemyNode and all six REAL bosses: a direct hit lands its folded value once (no second ×), the in-node entry is unchanged, the DEF dial applies after a boss's direct hit and its execute bypass reads the pre-vulnerability request (a discriminating survive / execute pair), and a Phase T2 hit lands whole. |
| catalog | **WR14** (new) | Executable view. The split rides exactly the ruled hits, and the five excluded constructions keep today's multiplier. The four chains are the only direct-entry callers. The entries skip only the in-node vulnerability. The punish window is on both entries with today's arithmetic. The Overcharge-free multiplier is today's sum minus Overcharge, token for token. |

### Production: as ruled, no new player-facing decision

| Change | Where |
|---|---|
| **S9, the icicle's crit (CL-117):** one crit roll for a shot, `rollShotCrit(directAttack:)`. Only a direct attack advances Calculated Strike (every 5th crits). `fireProjectile` calls it exactly as before; the absorbed pellets still return before any roll. **The replacement icicle now rolls it as a direct attack**: it crits (1.5× larger to the eye, same hitbox) and counts once toward Calculated Strike. **Its own shatter shards never crit** (`fireProjectile(…, rollsCrit: false)`). Iceburst's shards, echoes and fragments keep today's roll. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **S10, Shatter (CL-99 / CL-118):** one pure rule, `ShatterRule.outcome(…)`, holds today's gate, unchanged: a non-boss foe that is slowed, has total slow ≥ the threshold, and rolls below the chance. **A normal enemy dies outright**, as before; **an elite (mini-boss) takes the elite chunk** instead, 20% of max HP through the Anomaly helper. | `Systems/ShatterRule.swift` (new) |
| **Both hit paths ask that rule:** the gun's enemy hit and the Red Smile sweep. The shatter lands through the **plain** entry, so the vulnerability scales the chunk (a Frostbitten mini-boss loses 30%). **A kill or an execute ends the hit, as today; an elite that survives its chunk takes the rest of the hit** (CL-118a). There is **no rate limit** (CL-118b, a device watch). **The gun's Shatter exit charges Apex, then Erasure, once** (CL-118c, A4c F2's twin; the sweep's already did), and its kill **keeps the shot's Iceburst generation** (CL-118c). | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **Tuning:** Shatter's chance, threshold, Absolute Zero threshold and elite fraction move to `GameConfig.Chill`, with values unchanged (0.20 / 0.40 / 0.30 / 0.20; CL-118d). | `Config/GameConfig.swift`, `Systems/PlayerStats.swift`, `Systems/UpgradeManager.swift` |
| **Copy, Group 2 as approved Sep 28:** **G2-2** Shatter `Slowed foes may shatter (elites: 20% HP)` · **G2-3** Absolute Zero `Non-boss foes slow; shatters come easy` · **G2-4** Glacial Drift's detail `…slowing non-boss enemies by 50%…` · **G2-6** Whiteout's detail `…a snowman for 3s (elites for half as long). …`. No face changes. Polar Vortex's modal stays at 665pt (MD2), and every line fits its chip. | `Systems/UpgradeManager.swift` |

**Size:** GameScene +58/−29, ShatterRule +48 (new), GameConfig +9, UpgradeManager +6/−6, PlayerStats +2/−2.

**What the playtest will feel** (all ruled):
- **Shatter:** it no longer deletes mini-bosses. A slowed mini-boss loses 20% of its max HP per shatter (30% if Frostbitten; 40% if it is a melting snowman). The hit then goes on, with no cooldown, which is the CL-118b device watch.
- **The gun's Shatter kill:** it now charges The Hunter and Unstable like any landed hit.
- **Polar Vortex T4:** the icicle can crit (4 at base, 6 with Hemorrhage), so Gouge, Hemorrhage and Calculated Strike keep working after T4. Its shards can no longer crit.

### Production: as ruled, with the copy approved at its gates

| Change | Where |
|---|---|
| **CL-119 A1, Erasure:** a full meter lurches at the nearest foe, as before. **With no foe alive, it falls back to the arena boss, but only if the boss can be hit.** The Faceted Lie's vanish holds the charge. A boss lurch rolls only the effects that can reach it, each at the boss-class 50%: **Rift Burst** (the burst includes the boss: 50% ATK), **Damage Echo** (30% ATK after 0.35s, landing only if that same boss is still in the slot and hittable) and **Fracture** (+20% taken for 3s, its CL-116 channel, on a new scene-level game-time window, `bossFractureWindow`, that ticks with the boss status and resets for every new boss). A boss lurch is an activation, so T3's Rift Cannon advances. The normal-run behaviour, with any foe alive, is unchanged. The meter release and the Rift Cannon check moved into `releaseUnstableCharge()` / `fireRiftCannonIfDue()` (the same statements). | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **CL-120 B1′, Unstable Core:** the burst keeps its 4s cadence and ring, but **its self-hit lands only if it struck a live enemy**. Unbroken still covers it, and the Brace / Unbroken rescue order is unchanged. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **G1.12 / CL-127a, hittability for every targeter:** Chain Lightning's jumps, the Electro Pulse target, Sentry coils and their boss fallback (never the vanished Faceted Lie), and Relay Burn arcs (which had filtered nothing, not even dying bodies) now pick only `isHittable` bodies. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **CL-126, gun auto-aim:** it skips intangible targets, the enemy loop and the boss alike, falling through to the next visible hittable target or else holding fire. **The Skybeam-lassoed prey is included:** an intangible prey is never handed to the gun (`isHittableTarget`). CL-109's line-of-sight term for homing is still S13's. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **Copy:** Unstable Core's detail gains the approved `With nothing in range, it can't hurt you.` (477pt). Erasure's T1 rung is option A, with the modal at 665pt, unchanged. Everglow's detail is E1, `The pulse skips bosses; mini-bosses take half. Bosses and mini-bosses take half eruption damage.`, at 652pt, unchanged. All heights were measured with the harness's own model, and MD2 now pins both capstones. **CL-121's runtime is unchanged:** the pulse stays boss-excluded, and boss pulse tuning stays banked. | `Systems/UpgradeManager.swift` |

**Size:** GameScene +93/−19; UpgradeManager +3/−3.

**What the playtest will feel:**
- **Erasure in add-less boss fights:** the meter discharges at the boss instead of sitting full, with smaller effects. T3's beam now fires there too.
- **Unstable Core:** costs nothing with nobody near. Empty bursts no longer feed Everglow T3/T4's hit-taken growth either (a device-pass feel item, as the recon flagged).
- **Phased PaneStalkers and the vanished Faceted Lie:** chains, the pulse, coils, arcs and the gun all look past them. With nothing else hittable, the gun holds fire instead of shooting at nothing.

### Production, as ruled

| Seam | Change | Where |
|---|---|---|
| **S13** (CL-109, CL-123a/b) | **`ArenaGeometry.nearestVisible`**: the nearest candidate strictly within range whose exact segment (arc travel radius 3, `GameConfig.Geometry.arcTravelRadius`) is clear, else none. **Chain Lightning hops, Relay Burn arcs, the Electro Pulse and Sentry T1–T3** take it, so the Carrier blocks a jump (with no target in sight the pulse and coils stay charged). The coils' boss fallback needs the same clear line. **The T4 Lightning Network stays arena-wide** (CL-123b), and point bursts stay exempt under Ruling 1 (CL-123c, sidecar only). **Skybeam T3 homing** gets the gun's line-of-sight predicate, so an occluded prey falls through. | `Config/ArenaGeometry.swift`, `Config/GameConfig.swift`, `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **S14** (CL-124a) | **One path-tested shove:** `ArenaGeometry.pathShove` (Guard's exact test plus its bisection, lifted out unchanged) behind `GameScene.shove(_:along:by:)`. `guardShove` delegates to it with identical behaviour, and so does the Repulse flight's bisection. **The deer, both Brace-rescue sites, the boar's sideways shove, Erasure's Implosion pull, the Panda body check and roll, and the kaiju swipe** all go through it; the boar's charge itself passes as spectacle. **`EnemyNode.applyKnockback` is deleted.** | `Config/ArenaGeometry.swift`, `Scenes/GameScene.swift`, `Nodes/EnemyNode.swift` |
| **S14** (CL-109; CL-124b–d) | **The Vine Wall's edge push** moved out of `updateVineWall` into `applyVineWallEdge`, which runs beside the void-well pull BEFORE the frame's geometry resolve (the CL-77 precedent); T3's projectile catch stays where it was. **The Tree T5 lion** resolves out of the Carrier after each step (`GameConfig.Tree.lionFootprintRadius` = an ordinary body's; no route-steering). **Wildbloom flowers** are rejection-sampled off the Carrier AND inside the arena wall (`PlacementSampler.randomPoint(inDiscAt:…)`, margin `GameConfig.Growth.flowerRootMargin` 10; the garden centre is the fallback). **Rich Soil's ×1.22** (now `GameConfig.Growth.richSoilRadiusScale`) respects the 0.55R garden cap, so a later Growth pick never shrinks the garden. | `Scenes/GameScene.swift`, `Config/ArenaGeometry.swift`, `Config/GameConfig.swift` |
| **S15** (CL-59, CL-127d) | **Boss hazards reach the live (Harden-shrunk) body:** a `PlayerReachHazards` protocol (`playerHitRadius`) that the Quench Warden, Dynamo Choir and Faceted Lie adopt. The scene writes `playerStats.effectiveCollisionRadius` before every `boss?.update`, and their lanes, litany, plates and Pane burst read it. **The Quench Field wall clamp** reads the live radius too. Centre-point hazards are unchanged. | `Nodes/ArenaBossNode.swift`, the three bosses, `Scenes/GameScene.swift` |
| **S16** (G3.5 precondition) | **Settled:** a SpriteKit physics body DOES follow its node's `setScale`. On a ticking scene in a host window, a 12pt circle reached 24pt at ×2 and 6pt at ×0.5 (`a7b/s16/probe/`). The probe without a window never ticked, so it would have misled; it is recorded so nobody repeats it. EnemyNode's stale "never tracks setScale" comment is corrected (comment only). | `Nodes/EnemyNode.swift` |

**Size:** GameScene +106/−93, ArenaGeometry +57, GameConfig +13, EnemyNode +6/−10, ArenaBossNode +8, and Quench / Choir / Lie +6/−2, +6/−2 and +7/−3.

**Rich Soil copy check (ruled "copy check in the seam"):** I kept the face. "spreads wider" is true below the cap, and at the cap it is silent, as every Growth pick and the "🌱 Your ground spreads" hint already are. This is flagged for Brandon in case he wants the cap named.

- The lion and flowers never sit inside the Carrier, and flowers never sit past the wall.
- Rich Soil gardens never shrink.
- Harden now also shrinks the reach of boss hazards (lanes 25 → 22, plates 40 → 37).

### G3.5 — the visual/hitbox mismatch log (a docs deliverable; no collision change)

**How it was measured:**
- **Hit radius** = the `SKPhysicsBody` radius × |node scale|, now settled as the true runtime body.
- **Drawn extent** = the farthest drawn point at rest (strokes to their centreline; glow, UI and telegraphs excluded); [brackets] are animation peaks.
- **Method:** compiled by a read-only audit agent from source, and spot-checked (EnemyNode:381; GameConfig Enemy 14 / 12; Marchwarden 34 × 0.85; the Linekeeper head; the Anvilborn ring).
- ⚑ = drawn more than 2× the hit radius.

| Node | Hit radius (pt) | Drawn extent (pt) | Ratio | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| ⚑ Linekeeper | 12 | 45.5 (head ellipse top 3.25r; mast 39.2) | **3.79** [3.93 head pulse] | Linekeeper :52, :72-73, :87, :186, :195 |
| ⚑ Star Needle | 12 | 36.4 (shard tip 2.6r) | **3.03** [3.79 on lock] | StarNeedle :45-48, :113 |
| ⚑ Spurhound | 12 | 35.0 exhaust far edge (hull nose 26.6 = 2.22×) | **2.92** [4.03 on the lunge] | Spurhound :53-58, :80-81, :199, :215 |
| ⚑ Ramplate | 12 | 32.8 (face-plate corner 2.34r) | **2.73** | Ramplate :56-57, :79 (geometry footprint 25.2 is geometry only) |
| ⚑ Marchwarden | 28.9 (34 × 0.85) | 77.1 (mast corner; banner 73.2, plow 64.4) | **2.67** [3.55 banner pulse] | GameConfig :527; Marchwarden :207, :43, :121-146, :369, :404 |
| ⚑ Faceted Lie | 21 (30 × 0.7) | 48.7 (shard-plate vertex; core 18 is *smaller* than the hitbox) | **2.32** [2.97 plates apart] | FacetedLie :34, :295, :706, :151, :222-278 |
| ⚑ Static Halo | 12 | 25.25–27.25 (halo top, bob) | **2.10–2.27** | StaticHalo :67, :72, :77 |

**Found while logging (outside S16's scope, logged for the closeout audit, not fixed):**
- **The Anvilborn slam telegraph draws 35% larger in radius than its hit zone (82.25% larger in area).** The ring is a child of the ×1.35 node: it draws at 168.75pt, while the damage check uses 125pt (Anvilborn :117/:130 vs GameScene's slam check).
- **The Grounder pulse tell** is commented "exact damage radius", but it sits under the ×1.1 node: it draws 93.5pt against 85pt of damage (Grounder :113, GameConfig).

Both are telegraphs larger than the danger (generous, never unfair), and both are visual-only.

### Tests (focused; the exhaustive runs are deferred)

| Harness | Checks | What they prove |
|---|---|---|
| geometry | **G6, G7, G8** (new; `run.sh` now also extracts `Growth`) | **G6:** `nearestVisible` equals a brute force over 4,000 random Splitworks fields; an occluded nearer target loses to a visible one; a hidden-only field gives nil; strict range; ties to the first; an open arena is plain nearest. **G7:** `pathShove` lands exactly on a clear target; a blocked shove stops on its segment, never inside the Carrier, within one bisection step of the block (6,000 shoves); open arenas never stop. **G8:** the disc sampler is never in the Carrier or past the wall (by its margin), stays in its disc, and falls back when everything is rejected. |
| vulnerability | **RN7** (new) | On the six REAL bosses, exactly the Quench Warden, Dynamo Choir and Faceted Lie adopt `PlayerReachHazards`, start at the constant and take the live radius. |
| catalog | **WR19, WR20, WR21, WR22** (new); **WR18** re-pinned | **WR19:** every arc / hop site calls `nearestVisible` with the arc radius, the T4 exemption, the boss-fallback sight, homing LOS before its return, and the census. **WR20:** the exact shared `shove` and `guardShove` bodies, every knock site, `applyKnockback` / `lastFreePoint` gone app-wide, the Vine Wall edge pass immediately before the resolve, the lion resolve, the exact flower placement and the capped Rich Soil. **WR21:** the three bosses' `playerHitRadius` reads, the protocol, the write right before `boss?.update`, and both wall clamps live. **WR22:** the Unmade Star's one spawn path wipes before the monument arrives, and wave spawns and the mini-boss bell require no boss. **WR18** (S12's hittability): the four loops became `nearestVisible` calls, so the filters are re-pinned in their new form with the same hittability terms. |

## Build — geometry Units 4–5 (Arena 6 integration, with the settled audio) and S17 closeout — CHECKPOINT 19 → FINAL FREEZE 20 (Oct 1)

**Brandon's go is the S13–S16 message above:** "S13–S16, Geometry Units 4–5 including settled audio, and S17 closeout … After integration, run the consolidated regression and independent review, then stop for my release playtest … No new features, commits, pushes or ASC submission."

### Geometry Unit 4 — Arena 6 registered and presented (checkpoint 19, `179fb50ce08a3163`, 87 files)

| Item | Change |
|---|---|
| **Registration** | `ArenaConfig.splitworks` (formerly the Unit 1A shell) is arena 6 of `ArenaConfig.all`. The unlock registry then opens it on the Unmade Star's defeat, and the launch reconciliation heals existing Arena 5 clearers. The DEBUG shell seam is retired: the `splitworksShell` and `splitworksShellOverrideID` symbols, `GeometryDebug.forceSplitworksShell` and `DevSeams.shellIndex` are gone, and the DEV ARENA pick simply cycles all six. The void harness's EH2 and geometry's GR0 pins follow the rename. |
| **Boss Mode** | A `BossRegistry` entry for the Marchwarden (home arena 5, `.arena`) and the gauntlet spawner `case "marchwarden"`. `swapArena` rebuilds the Splitworks geometry and enters at its safe anchor. |
| **The Marchwarden's defeat** | Records the bestiary, `registerArenaBossDefeat("marchwarden")` and **Marchworn** (`unlockEarned("spark_marchworn")`). |
| **Bestiary** | Four families with stable ids (`spurhound`, `linekeeper`, `ramplate`, `marchwarden`), names, colours, the Marchwarden as a boss, and the design lock's lines verbatim. Live mapping comes from the three enemy classes (before this, they were booked as `melee`), and each has a live portrait. Every bestiary line fits the live wrapper (≤ 3 lines at 39 characters on a 375pt phone). |
| **Marchworn** | Family "The Broken March" (not secret); an earned re-tint (`0x55514C` iron, `0x3F8F8A` teal halo, `0xFFB070` ember heart, `0xE8E0D0` eyes, `0xD9772E` trail). Blurb: "Iron that kept marching after the road broke. Earned in Arena 6." |
| **Presentation** | `buildSplitworksMotif`: procession rails into the wreck, route chevrons, gate masonry, broken signal standards. `addCarrierHullDetail`: ribs, an ember break line and a cab, all inside the collision outline. Before this, arena 6 fell through to the Crucible motif. |
| **Sound** | Five procedural `AudioManager` cues from the shared synth helpers (`spurhoundWhine`, `linekeeperAim`, `ramplateBrace`, `wardenMuster`, `splitworksHorn`), fired from the scene through node callbacks (`onAimStart` and `onMusterCalled` are new; the others already existed). The horn plays once as an Arena 6 run opens. |
| **The BGM deck** | `BGMDeck` (pure and generic) plus a rewritten `MusicManager`: one deck of every `bgm_*` track; app-session lifetime; continuous across contexts; resume after the toggle, an interruption or the foreground; no back-to-back repeat at the boundary; a failed start is dropped. The 20 recordings are imported under their verified names, byte-checked (71,528,558 bytes). The built app holds exactly those 20 (20 distinct Suno IDs, no alternate encodings), and the raw downloads and manifests are untouched. The BGM README is rewritten. |

**New checks:**
- **`tools/bgm-harness`** (the 14th harness, BD1–BD5 plus BA1), which executes the real `BGMDeck`: once per cycle, refill only at the boundary, no boundary repeat (7,200 boundaries), failed-start drop, empty / one-track decks, and an unbiased opening track. BA1 checks the bundle against the pinned import map.
- **Catalog CT9** (bestiary) and **WR23** (registration, Boss Mode, the defeat line, bestiary mapping and portraits, Marchworn, cues, and the deck wiring incl. `setContext` never drawing while a song exists).
- **Mutants U4-1…10.** `mutations.py` now **symlinks** `Sparkforge/Audio` into mutant copies (read-only; `rmtree` unlinks the symlink and never follows it), so BA1 runs everywhere without 71MB per copy.

### Geometry Unit 5 — integration and closeout

The reusable record is **`docs/arena6-production-closeout.md`**. It holds the 10-step "shipping an arena" checklist, Arena 6 as shipped, sound, the integration checks and the open items.
- **Compatibility:** clean builds; Boss Mode loads the home arena.
- **Progression:** the registry arithmetic is unchanged.
- **Persistence:** add-only. A source diff of every `sf_` / `sparkforge_` literal shows none removed or renamed.
- **Telemetry:** the existing geometry counters cover every Arena 6 verb.
- **Performance:** about 52 static floor nodes, a device watch.
- **Copy:** the bestiary is fitted; the Marchworn blurb is for Brandon.

- **Stale sidecar lines fixed**, each verified against the live copy first:
  - Apex "injured foes";

- **The Atlas** regenerated and verified (80 cards) into `a7b/s17/sparkforge-card-atlas.html`. The root Atlas file is user-owned and untouched. **Its republish is held for after the release playtest**, with the commit.

### The consolidated regression and the independent review (Oct 1)

**How it ran.**
- Final freeze 20 (`5f48e7e84f9bc68a`, 91 files) was cut first. All 14 harnesses passed on it (927/927), and the full mutation suite started.
- **Three independent reviewers** then read the work with fresh context. They were read-only Claude subagents, not the Codex Reviewer thread, which isn't reachable from this session:
  - **A** (S8–S12): **PASS WITH FINDINGS**;
  - **B** (S13–S16 plus geometry Units 4–5): **PASS WITH FINDINGS**;
  - **C**, re-reviewing the fixes for A and B: **PASS WITH FINDINGS**, with no major findings and "every fix does fix its finding".
- The suite was stopped twice before finishing, so the release tree wouldn't be measured on stale code. On freeze 20 it had reached 188/188 killed; on freeze 21, 52/52. Both partial logs are kept in `a7b/final/`.

| Finding | Severity | Disposition |
|---|---|---|
| **A-M1** CL-114a's "Overcharge never below today" holds at the shot's BASE only. The integer steps after it (a non-integer crit multiplier such as Deadeye ×2.1 / ×3.1, Lucky Break, Forge offense ≥ ×1.25, Killing Stroke) act on the Overcharge-free base, so a hit can land ~1 below today at an integer crossing. | MAJOR (ruling) | **Brandon, Oct 1: "Ship as built, bank a fix."** The truth was corrected in the DH10 label and the `v13_overcharge` sidecar (crit added after re-review C), and the remedy is banked for v2.1.x/v2.2. **This corrects S8's own claim above** ("an Iron Skin / Everglow integer crossing never drops below its old floor"): that holds for the base, not for every hit. |
| A-m1: the sidecar's crosscutting Hittability rule claimed every targeter | MINOR | Reworded to the named set; the targeters still filtering on dying alone are listed. |
| A-N1 Erasure's normal lurch can still pick a phased foe · A-N2 the approved Chill copy reads as if the arena-wide slow qualifies for Shatter (only a foe's own slow does) · A-N3 a surviving elite's continuing hit can stack Anomaly's chunk · A-N4 sub-1 scaled shots gain nothing from Overcharge · A-N5 `ObjectIdentifier` reuse | NOTE | N4 is now in the sidecar. N1–N3 are **device watches** for the release playtest. N2 is the approved copy, kept as approved. |
| **B-M1** After the Marchwarden falls, Arena 6's title card showed "★ THE STAR IS UNMADE ★" (the last-arena branch was hard-coded) | MAJOR | `ArenaConfig.finalFelledLine`. **Brandon's pick: "★ THE MARCH IS BROKEN ★".** The Star keeps its line. WR23 and U4-11 cover it. |
| B-m1: the Boss Mode modal overflows an iPhone SE with six bosses | MINOR | The whole modal scales to fit inside the safe area (re-review C) (U4-21). |
| B-m2: one session-wide `play()` refusal dropped every track | MINOR | A refused start `restore`s the track for the next transport event. After re-review C: the same track refused twice in a row is dropped (`BGMDeck.refused`), so a single bad file can't wedge the deck (BD6, BD6b, U4-13, U4-17). |
| B-m3: MusicManager's rules were only text-pinned | MINOR | **`BGMPolicy`**, a pure transport table executed by bgm BP1–BP6. MusicManager routes every event through `perform`. Re-review C's pin gaps are closed: the pause body, the `.ended` filter and decode-error routing (U4-9/10/15/18/19/20). |
| B-m4: a Boss Mode swap left gardens, flowers and the Tree in the new arena's Carrier | MINOR | `clampLooseNodesToArena` now clamps them too (U4-12). Re-review C nit: the clamp is the orb rule, so a garden straddling the Carrier can leave a flower just outside its disc. That's cosmetic; nothing roots inside a solid. |
| B-N1 the user-audio check ran at every song change · B-N3 stale "pool" comments · **B-N4 the Marchwarden's Column Advances never fired** (it searched `worldNode` for bodies that live in `arenaLayer`; a pre-existing geometry Unit 3 bug) · B-N5 the mutation symlink · B-N6 the shove radius | NOTE | N1 → a first-start-only `everStarted` (U4-14). N3 fixed. **N4 fixed** (U4-16). N5 → `run_one` refuses any mutant under a linked directory. N6 is the Guard precedent, unchanged. |
| C-nits: README / BD4 wording, the U4-11 name, the Column Advances comment, the ArenaConfig comment placement, BD6's restore guard | NIT | All fixed. |

**Brandon's answers (Oct 1):**
- Overcharge: ship as built and bank a fix.
- The Arena 6 victory line: "★ THE MARCH IS BROKEN ★".
- The Marchworn blurb: "Iron that kept marching after the road broke. Earned in Arena 6."

### For the release playtest (Brandon's device gate; it covers S6–S17 and geometry Units 4–5)

1. **Arena 6.** Unlocks after the Unmade Star.
   - **The floor:** rails into the wreck, chevrons, gates, standards.
   - **The Carrier:** its hull ribs and ember break.
   - **The opening horn** and the four tells: the Spurhound whine, the Linekeeper aim tone, the Ramplate brace, the Marchwarden muster call.
   - **The Marchwarden's Column Advances**, which now actually fires.
   - **Marchworn** on the first clear, and **"★ THE MARCH IS BROKEN ★"** on the title card.
   - **The four bestiary entries.**
2. **BGM.** Listen for:
   - one song carrying across title, run and boss, then the next;
   - no repeat across a reshuffle;
   - BGM OFF → ON resuming the same song;
   - a phone call, Control Center or backgrounding resuming it too.
3. **Boss Mode.** With six bosses: the Marchwarden stage (Carrier, safe anchor) and the panel's fit on a small phone.
4. **The A7b device watches:**
   - Shatter on mini-bosses (no cooldown);
   - the crit icicle;
   - Erasure in add-less boss fights;
   - Unstable Core with Everglow;
   - phased and vanished targets;
   - arcs, homing and knocks at the Carrier;
   - Harden's shrunk boss hazards;
   - Overcharge with Forge Path builds (the banked loss).
5. **Performance:** the Splitworks floor's ~52 static nodes, judged on device.

- any push;

## Post-acceptance fix — BGM holds (Oct 1, a follow-up commit after the release commit `e9138ad`)

> BGM was playing as soon as the app was opened, and it persisted during pauses, and even after the app was minimized but left running. I think we'll need to fix that.

**Cause.** The playtest gate's "backgrounding resumes it" item exposed a missing half. `MusicManager` resumed on `didBecomeActive` but never paused itself:
- not on `didEnterBackground`: iOS suspends a backgrounded app soon after, but a debugger-attached run isn't suspended;
- not for the run's pause menu.

There was a worse edge: an interruption that **ended while the app was in the background** resumed the song there. The pre-Unit 4 manager had the same gap, but no tracks were bundled until Unit 4, so it never sounded.

**Fix.** `MusicManager` keeps **holds** (`background`, `pauseMenu`). The song plays only when no hold stands.
- **Background.** The `didEnterBackground` hold pauses the song at once; a fade would never finish before suspension. `didBecomeActive` lifts it and the same song fades back in.
- **Pause menu.** `pauseGame` sets the hold, which fades the song out and keeps its place. `resumeGame` and `returnToTitle` lift it; `returnToTitle` is the scene's only exit, so a hold can't outlive its run.
- **The policy.** `BGMPolicy.action(..., held:, ...)` adds the `held` / `released` events. A hold pauses in place, like BGM OFF. While any hold stands, nothing resumes, starts or draws: not an ended interruption, not the foreground, not a finished track. A track that ends under a hold is drawn fresh when the hold lifts.
- **The delayed pause.** After the fade, it now asks `shouldBeSilent` (OFF or held) instead of OFF alone.
- **Unchanged.** Level-up cards, synergy reveals and the other in-run modals do not hold the song; only the player's pause menu does. The title keeps its music (the settled continuous plan; Brandon confirmed Oct 1: "I'm fine with the title screen opening with BGM - that's awesome").

**Proof.**
- **Harnesses.** bgm BP2/BP3 now include `.released`. The new **BP7** checks that a hold pauses, and that while held nothing resumes, starts or draws. Catalog WR23 is re-pinned (the `held:` call, the hold-aware delayed pause, the foreground handler). The new **WR24** pins the holds, the background handler, `setGamePaused`, and the three scene call sites, with `presentScene` once.
- **Mutants.** U4-15 is re-pointed (`.released` joined its row). Ten new mutants, U4-22…U4-31, cover the two playtest bugs, a hold that never lifts (Resume, quit to the title), the foreground lifting the menu hold, a hold that lets playback restart, and the fade-instead-of-stop. **The U4 subset kills 31/31.**
- **Results.** **937/937 across the 14 harnesses** (935 + BP7 + WR24). The Debug build is clean, with warnings identical to the freeze-22 baseline. The full mutation suite (480) was not rerun; only the U4 subset was.
- **Device watch:** pause, Resume, quit from pause, minimize and return, both with and without the pause menu up.
- **Committed and pushed** on Brandon's go (Oct 1: "we're ok to commit/push"), as one follow-up commit of these eight files. His doc task's four files, the staged icon swap and `Info.plist` stay out.
